```
1→"""Centralized score update display for all state-changing commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import format_strict_target_progress
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_score_update(
    12→    state: dict,
    13→    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    14→    *,
    15→    config: dict | None = None,
    16→    label: str = "Scores",
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print score quartet with deltas and strict target progress.
    19→
    20→    Args:
    21→        state: Current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    22→        prev: ScoreSnapshot taken before the operation.
    23→        config: Project config dict (needed for target_strict_score).
    24→        label: Prefix label (default "Scores").
    25→    """
    26→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    27→    if (
    28→        new.overall is None
    29→        or new.objective is None
    30→        or new.strict is None
    31→        or new.verified is None
    32→    ):
    33→        print(colorize(f"\n  {label} unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    34→        return
    35→
    36→    overall_s, overall_c = format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
    37→    objective_s, objective_c = format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
    38→    strict_s, strict_c = format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
    39→    verified_s, verified_c = format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)
    40→
    41→    print(
    42→        f"\n  {label}: "
    43→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_s}", overall_c)
    44→        + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_s}", objective_c)
    45→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_s}", strict_c)
    46→        + colorize(f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_s}", verified_c)
    47→    )
    48→
    49→    # Show strict target progress when config is available
    50→    if config is not None:
    51→        from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    52→        from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core import NarrativeContext
    53→
    54→        narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    55→            state, context=NarrativeContext(command="score_update"),
    56→        )
    57→        strict_target = narrative.get("strict_target")
    58→        if strict_target:
    59→            lines, _target, _gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
    60→            for message, style in lines:
    61→                print(colorize(message, style))
    62→
    63→
    64→__all__ = ["print_score_update"]
    65→
```

```
1→"""Target-score normalization helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     6→
     7→
     8→def coerce_target_score(value: object, *, fallback: float = 95.0) -> float:
     9→    """Normalize target score-like values to a safe [0, 100] float."""
    10→    if is_numeric(fallback):
    11→        fallback_value = float(fallback)
    12→    else:
    13→        fallback_value = 95.0
    14→
    15→    if is_numeric(value):
    16→        parsed = float(value)
    17→    elif isinstance(value, str):
    18→        text = value.strip()
    19→        if not text:
    20→            parsed = fallback_value
    21→        else:
    22→            try:
    23→                parsed = float(text)
    24→            except ValueError:
    25→                parsed = fallback_value
    26→    else:
    27→        parsed = fallback_value
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, parsed))
    29→
    30→
    31→def target_strict_score_from_config(
    32→    config: dict | None, *, fallback: float = 95.0
    33→) -> float:
    34→    """Read and normalize target strict score from config."""
    35→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    36→        raw = config.get("target_strict_score", fallback)
    37→    else:
    38→        raw = fallback
    39→    return coerce_target_score(raw, fallback=fallback)
    40→
    41→
```

```
1→"""Shared strict-target progress rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→def format_strict_target_progress(
     7→    strict_target: dict | None,
     8→) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, str]], float | None, float | None]:
     9→    """Return display lines and numeric values for strict target progress."""
    10→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    11→    if not isinstance(strict_target, dict):
    12→        return lines, None, None
    13→
    14→    warning = strict_target.get("warning")
    15→    if isinstance(warning, str) and warning.strip():
    16→        lines.append((f"  * {warning.strip()}", "yellow"))
    17→
    18→    target = strict_target.get("target")
    19→    current = strict_target.get("current")
    20→    gap = strict_target.get("gap")
    21→    state = strict_target.get("state")
    22→    if not isinstance(target, int | float):
    23→        return lines, None, None
    24→    target_f = float(target)
    25→
    26→    if not isinstance(current, int | float):
    27→        lines.append(
    28→            (
    29→                f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · current strict score unavailable",
    30→                "dim",
    31→            )
    32→        )
    33→        return lines, target_f, None
    34→
    35→    current_f = float(current)
    36→    gap_f = (
    37→        float(gap) if isinstance(gap, int | float) else round(target_f - current_f, 1)
    38→    )
    39→    if state == "below":
    40→        lines.append((
    41→            f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · currently {current_f:.1f}/100 ({gap_f:.1f} below target)",
    42→            "yellow",
    43→        ))
    44→    elif state == "above":
    45→        lines.append((
    46→            f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · currently {current_f:.1f}/100 ({abs(gap_f):.1f} above target)",
    47→            "green",
    48→        ))
    49→    else:
    50→        lines.append((f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · on target", "green"))
    51→
    52→    return lines, target_f, gap_f
    53→
```

```
1→"""Shared helpers for scan command orchestration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify import languages as lang_api
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS, read_file_text
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→
    13→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    14→
    15→
    16→def _audit_excluded_dirs(
    17→    exclusions: tuple[str, ...],
    18→    scanned_files: list[str],
    19→    project_root: Path,
    20→) -> list[dict]:
    21→    """Check if any --exclude directory has zero references from scanned code.
    22→
    23→    Returns findings for directories that appear stale (no file references them).
    24→    """
    25→    if not exclusions:
    26→        return []
    27→
    28→    candidate_dirs = [
    29→        ex_dir
    30→        for ex_dir in exclusions
    31→        if ex_dir not in DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS and (project_root / ex_dir).is_dir()
    32→    ]
    33→    if not candidate_dirs:
    34→        return []
    35→
    36→    # Read each scanned file once and mark any exclusions observed in content.
    37→    unresolved = set(candidate_dirs)
    38→    for filepath in scanned_files:
    39→        if not unresolved:
    40→            break
    41→        abs_path = (
    42→            filepath if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else str(project_root / filepath)
    43→        )
    44→        content = read_file_text(abs_path)
    45→        if content is None:
    46→            logger.debug(
    47→                "Skipping unreadable file %s while auditing exclusions", filepath
    48→            )
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        matched = {ex_dir for ex_dir in unresolved if ex_dir in content}
    52→        if matched:
    53→            unresolved.difference_update(matched)
    54→
    55→    stale_findings = []
    56→    for ex_dir in candidate_dirs:
    57→        if ex_dir not in unresolved:
    58→            continue
    59→        stale_findings.append(
    60→            state_mod.make_finding(
    61→                "stale_exclude",
    62→                ex_dir,
    63→                ex_dir,
    64→                tier=4,
    65→                confidence="low",
    66→                summary=(
    67→                    f"Excluded directory '{ex_dir}' has 0 references from scanned code — "
    68→                    "may be stale"
    69→                ),
    70→                detail={"directory": ex_dir, "references": 0},
    71→            )
    72→        )
    73→    return stale_findings
    74→
    75→
    76→def _collect_codebase_metrics(lang, path: Path) -> dict | None:
    77→    """Collect LOC/file/directory counts for the configured language."""
    78→    if not lang or not lang.file_finder:
    79→        return None
    80→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    81→    total_loc = 0
    82→    dirs = set()
    83→    for filepath in files:
    84→        try:
    85→            total_loc += len(Path(filepath).read_text().splitlines())
    86→            dirs.add(str(Path(filepath).parent))
    87→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
    88→            logger.debug(
    89→                "Skipping unreadable file %s while collecting scan metrics: %s",
    90→                filepath,
    91→                exc,
    92→            )
    93→    return {
    94→        "total_files": len(files),
    95→        "total_loc": total_loc,
    96→        "total_directories": len(dirs),
    97→    }
    98→
    99→
   100→def _resolve_scan_profile(profile: str | None, lang) -> str:
   101→    """Resolve effective scan profile from CLI and language defaults."""
   102→    if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"}:
   103→        return profile
   104→    default_profile = getattr(lang, "default_scan_profile", "full") if lang else "full"
   105→    if default_profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"}:
   106→        return default_profile
   107→    return "full"
   108→
   109→
   110→def _effective_include_slow(include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> bool:
   111→    """Determine whether slow phases should run for this profile."""
   112→    return include_slow and profile != "ci"
   113→
   114→
   115→def _format_hidden_by_detector(hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]) -> str:
   116→    """Format hidden findings counts for terminal output."""
   117→    return ", ".join(f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items())
   118→
   119→
   120→def _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   121→    args, lang, path: Path, metrics: dict | None
   122→) -> None:
   123→    """Warn when user explicitly selected a language but scan found zero files."""
   124→    explicit_lang = getattr(args, "lang", None)
   125→    if not explicit_lang or not lang or not metrics:
   126→        return
   127→    if metrics.get("total_files", 0) > 0:
   128→        return
   129→
   130→    suggestion = " Omit `--lang` to auto-detect."
   131→    root = path if path.is_dir() else path.parent
   132→    try:
   133→        detected = lang_api.auto_detect_lang(root)
   134→    except ValueError as exc:
   135→        detected = None
   136→        logger.debug(
   137→            "Auto-detect failed while warning for explicit lang on %s: %s", root, exc
   138→        )
   139→    else:
   140→        if detected and detected != lang.name:
   141→            suggestion = (
   142→                f" Detected `{detected}` for this path — use `--lang {detected}` "
   143→                "or omit `--lang`."
   144→            )
   145→
   146→    print(
   147→        colorize(
   148→            f"  ⚠ No {lang.name} source files found under `{path}`.{suggestion}",
   149→            "yellow",
   150→        )
   151→    )
   152→
   153→
   154→def format_delta(value: float, prev: float | None) -> tuple[str, str]:
   155→    """Return (delta_str, color) for a score change."""
   156→    delta = value - prev if prev is not None else 0
   157→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta != 0 else ""
   158→    color = "green" if delta > 0 else ("red" if delta < 0 else "dim")
   159→    return delta_str, color
   160→
   161→
   162→__all__ = [
   163→    "_audit_excluded_dirs",
   164→    "_collect_codebase_metrics",
   165→    "_effective_include_slow",
   166→    "format_delta",
   167→    "_format_hidden_by_detector",
   168→    "_resolve_scan_profile",
   169→    "_warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files",
   170→]
   171→
```

```
1→"""Score and diff summary output for scan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import (
    10→    format_strict_target_progress,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_llm import _is_agent_environment
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    19→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    20→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    21→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    22→        return 0
    23→
    24→    streak = 0
    25→    for entry in reversed(history):
    26→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→            break
    28→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    29→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    30→            break
    31→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    32→            break
    33→        streak += 1
    34→    return streak
    35→
    36→
    37→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    38→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    39→    diff_parts = []
    40→    if diff["new"]:
    41→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    42→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    43→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    44→    if diff["reopened"]:
    45→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    46→    if diff_parts:
    47→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    48→    else:
    49→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    50→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    51→        print(
    52→            colorize(
    53→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    54→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
    55→                "yellow",
    56→            )
    57→        )
    58→
    59→
    60→def show_score_delta(
    61→    state: dict,
    62→    prev_overall: float | None,
    63→    prev_objective: float | None,
    64→    prev_strict: float | None,
    65→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
    66→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
    67→):
    68→    """Print the canonical score quartet with deltas."""
    69→    stats = state["stats"]
    70→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    71→
    72→    if (
    73→        new.overall is None
    74→        or new.objective is None
    75→        or new.strict is None
    76→        or new.verified is None
    77→    ):
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
    81→                "yellow",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
    87→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
    88→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
    89→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
    90→    print(
    91→        "  Scores: "
    92→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
    93→        + colorize(
    94→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
    95→            objective_color,
    96→        )
    97→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
    98→        + colorize(
    99→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   100→            verified_color,
   101→        )
   102→    )
   103→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   104→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   105→    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
   106→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   107→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   108→    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
   109→        print(
   110→            colorize(
   111→                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
   112→                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
   113→                "yellow",
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→
   117→    # Score legend — shown on first scan or when strict gap is significant
   118→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   119→    if scan_count <= 1 or gap > 10 or _is_agent_environment():
   120→        print(colorize("  Score guide:", "dim"))
   121→        print(colorize("    overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)", "dim"))
   122→        print(colorize("    objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)", "dim"))
   123→        print(colorize("    strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star", "dim"))
   124→        print(colorize("    verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes", "dim"))
   125→
   126→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
   127→    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
   128→        status = integrity.get("status")
   129→        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   130→        target = integrity.get("target_score")
   131→        if status == "penalized":
   132→            print(
   133→                colorize(
   134→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   135→                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   136→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
   137→                    "red",
   138→                )
   139→            )
   140→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   141→            if streak >= 2:
   142→                print(
   143→                    colorize(
   144→                        "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   145→                        "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   146→                        "yellow",
   147→                    )
   148→                )
   149→        elif status == "warn":
   150→            print(
   151→                colorize(
   152→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   153→                    f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   154→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}). Re-review recommended.",
   155→                    "yellow",
   156→                )
   157→            )
   158→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   159→            if streak >= 2:
   160→                print(
   161→                    colorize(
   162→                        "    This warning has repeated. Prefer "
   163→                        "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   164→                        "or run a blind reviewer pass before import.",
   165→                        "yellow",
   166→                )
   167→            )
   168→
   169→
   170→def show_concern_count(state: dict, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:
   171→    """Print concern count if any exist."""
   172→    try:
   173→        from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   174→
   175→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   176→        if concerns:
   177→            print(
   178→                colorize(
   179→                    f"  {len(concerns)} potential design concern{'s' if len(concerns) != 1 else ''}"
   180→                    " (run `show concerns` to view)",
   181→                    "cyan",
   182→                )
   183→            )
   184→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   185→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   186→
   187→
   188→def show_strict_target_progress(strict_target: dict | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   189→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   190→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
   191→    for message, style in lines:
   192→        print(colorize(message, style))
   193→    return target, gap
   194→
   195→
   196→__all__ = ["show_concern_count", "show_diff_summary", "show_score_delta", "show_strict_target_progress"]
   197→
```

```
1→"""Scan reporting: score breakdown, delta helpers, and detector progress."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

    15→

    17→class _NarrativeMod(Protocol):

    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→# Breakdown helpers (from scan_reporting_breakdown)
    29→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→
    31→
    32→def dimension_bar(score: float, *, colorize_fn, bar_len: int = 15) -> str:
    33→    """Render a score bar consistent with scan detector bars."""
    34→    filled = max(0, min(bar_len, round(score / 100 * bar_len)))
    35→    if score >= 98:
    36→        return colorize_fn("█" * bar_len, "green")
    37→    if score >= 93:
    38→        return colorize_fn("█" * filled, "green") + colorize_fn(
    39→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    40→        )
    41→    return colorize_fn("█" * filled, "yellow") + colorize_fn(
    42→        "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    43→    )
    44→
    45→
    46→def show_score_model_breakdown(
    47→    state: dict,
    48→    *,
    49→    scoring_mod,
    50→    colorize_fn,
    51→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    52→) -> None:
    53→    """Show score recipe and weighted drags."""
    54→    if dim_scores is None:
    55→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    56→    if not dim_scores:
    57→        return
    58→
    59→    breakdown = scoring_mod.compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
    60→    mech_frac = float(breakdown.get("mechanical_fraction", 1.0) or 0.0)
    61→    subj_frac = float(breakdown.get("subjective_fraction", 0.0) or 0.0)
    62→    mech_avg = float(breakdown.get("mechanical_avg", 100.0) or 100.0)
    63→    subj_avg_raw = breakdown.get("subjective_avg")
    64→    subj_avg = float(subj_avg_raw) if isinstance(subj_avg_raw, int | float) else None
    65→    entries = [
    66→        entry for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []) if isinstance(entry, dict)
    67→    ]
    68→    if not entries:
    69→        return
    70→
    71→    print(colorize_fn("  Score recipe:", "dim"))
    72→    if subj_avg is None or subj_frac <= 0.0:
    73→        print(colorize_fn("    overall = 100% mechanical", "dim"))
    74→        print(colorize_fn(f"    Mechanical pool average: {mech_avg:.1f}%", "dim"))
    75→    elif mech_frac <= 0.0:
    76→        print(colorize_fn("    overall = 100% subjective", "dim"))
    77→        print(colorize_fn(f"    Subjective pool average: {subj_avg:.1f}%", "dim"))
    78→    else:
    79→        print(
    80→            colorize_fn(
    81→                f"    overall = {mech_frac * 100:.0f}% mechanical + {subj_frac * 100:.0f}% subjective",
    82→                "dim",
    83→            )
    84→        )
    85→        print(
    86→            colorize_fn(
    87→                f"    Pool averages: mechanical {mech_avg:.1f}% · subjective {subj_avg:.1f}%",
    88→                "dim",
    89→            )
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    drags = sorted(
    93→        [
    94→            entry
    95→            for entry in entries
    96→            if float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0.0) or 0.0) > 0.01
    97→        ],
    98→        key=lambda entry: -float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0.0) or 0.0),
    99→    )
   100→    if drags:
   101→        print(colorize_fn("    Biggest weighted drags:", "dim"))
   102→        for entry in drags[:5]:
   103→            name = str(entry.get("name", "unknown"))
   104→            score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0) or 0.0)
   105→            drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0.0) or 0.0)
   106→            pool = str(entry.get("pool", "unknown"))
   107→            pool_share = float(entry.get("pool_share", 0.0) or 0.0) * 100
   108→            print(
   109→                colorize_fn(
   110→                    f"      - {name}: -{drag:.2f} pts "
   111→                    f"(score {score:.1f}%, {pool_share:.1f}% of {pool} pool)",
   112→                    "dim",
   113→                )
   114→            )
   115→    print()
   116→
   117→
   118→def show_dimension_deltas(
   119→    prev: dict,
   120→    current: dict,
   121→    *,
   122→    scoring_mod,
   123→    colorize_fn,
   124→) -> None:
   125→    """Show which dimensions changed between scans (health and strict)."""
   126→    moved = []
   127→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   128→        p = prev.get(dim.name, {})
   129→        n = current.get(dim.name, {})
   130→        if not p or not n:
   131→            continue
   132→        old_score = p.get("score", 100)
   133→        new_score = n.get("score", 100)
   134→        old_strict = p.get("strict", old_score)
   135→        new_strict = n.get("strict", new_score)
   136→        delta = new_score - old_score
   137→        strict_delta = new_strict - old_strict
   138→        if abs(delta) >= 0.1 or abs(strict_delta) >= 0.1:
   139→            moved.append(
   140→                (
   141→                    dim.name,
   142→                    old_score,
   143→                    new_score,
   144→                    delta,
   145→                    old_strict,
   146→                    new_strict,
   147→                    strict_delta,
   148→                )
   149→            )
   150→
   151→    if not moved:
   152→        return
   153→
   154→    print(colorize_fn("  Moved:", "dim"))
   155→    for name, old, new, delta, old_s, new_s, s_delta in sorted(
   156→        moved, key=lambda item: item[3]
   157→    ):
   158→        sign = "+" if delta > 0 else ""
   159→        color = "green" if delta > 0 else "red"
   160→        strict_str = ""
   161→        if abs(s_delta) >= 0.1:
   162→            s_sign = "+" if s_delta > 0 else ""
   163→            strict_str = colorize_fn(
   164→                f"  strict: {old_s:.1f}→{new_s:.1f}% ({s_sign}{s_delta:.1f}%)",
   165→                "dim",
   166→            )
   167→        print(
   168→            colorize_fn(
   169→                f"    {name:<22} {old:.1f}% → {new:.1f}%  ({sign}{delta:.1f}%)", color
   170→            )
   171→            + strict_str
   172→        )
   173→    print()
   174→
   175→
   176→def show_low_dimension_hints(
   177→    dim_scores: dict,
   178→    *,
   179→    scoring_mod,
   180→    colorize_fn,
   181→) -> None:
   182→    """Show actionable hints for dimensions below 50%."""
   183→    static_names = {dim.name for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS}
   184→
   185→    mechanical_hints = {
   186→        "File health": "run `desloppify show structural` — split large files",
   187→        "Code quality": "run `desloppify show smells` — fix code smells",
   188→        "Duplication": "run `desloppify show dupes` — deduplicate functions",
   189→        "Test health": "add tests for uncovered files: `desloppify show test_coverage`",
   190→        "Security": "run `desloppify show security` — fix security issues",
   191→    }
   192→
   193→    low = []
   194→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   195→        strict = data.get("strict", data.get("score", 100))
   196→        if strict < 50:
   197→            hint = (
   198→                mechanical_hints.get(name, "run `desloppify show` for details")
   199→                if name in static_names
   200→                else "run `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to assess"
   201→            )
   202→            low.append((name, strict, hint))
   203→
   204→    if not low:
   205→        return
   206→
   207→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
   208→    print(colorize_fn("  Needs attention:", "yellow"))
   209→    for name, score, hint in low:
   210→        print(colorize_fn(f"    {name} ({score:.0f}%) — {hint}", "yellow"))
   211→    print()
   212→
   213→
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→# Detector progress (from scan_reporting_progress)
   216→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   217→
   218→
   219→def show_detector_progress(
   220→    state: dict,
   221→    *,
   222→    state_mod: _StateMod,
   223→    narrative_mod: _NarrativeMod,
   224→    registry_mod: _RegistryMod,
   225→    colorize_fn: Callable[[str, str], str],
   226→) -> None:
   227→    """Show per-detector progress bars."""
   228→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(state["findings"], state.get("scan_path"))
   229→    if not findings:
   230→        return
   231→
   232→    by_detector: dict[str, dict] = {}
   233→    for finding in findings.values():
   234→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
   235→        if detector in narrative_mod.STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   236→            detector = "structural"
   237→        if detector not in by_detector:
   238→            by_detector[detector] = {"open": 0, "total": 0}
   239→        by_detector[detector]["total"] += 1
   240→        if finding["status"] == "open":
   241→            by_detector[detector]["open"] += 1
   242→
   243→    detector_order = [
   244→        registry_mod.DETECTORS[d].display
   245→        for d in registry_mod.display_order()
   246→        if d in registry_mod.DETECTORS
   247→    ]
   248→    order_map = {display: i for i, display in enumerate(detector_order)}
   249→    sorted_dets = sorted(by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: order_map.get(item[0], 99))
   250→
   251→    print(colorize_fn("  Detector progress (open findings by detector):", "dim"))
   252→    print(colorize_fn("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"))
   253→    bar_len = 15
   254→    for detector, data in sorted_dets:
   255→        total = data["total"]
   256→        open_count = data["open"]
   257→        addressed = total - open_count
   258→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   259→
   260→        filled = round(pct / 100 * bar_len)
   261→        if pct == 100:
   262→            bar = colorize_fn("█" * bar_len, "green")
   263→        elif open_count <= 2:
   264→            bar = colorize_fn("█" * filled, "green") + colorize_fn(
   265→                "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
   266→            )
   267→        else:
   268→            bar = colorize_fn("█" * filled, "yellow") + colorize_fn(
   269→                "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
   270→            )
   271→
   272→        det_label = detector.replace("_", " ").ljust(18)
   273→        open_str = (
   274→            colorize_fn(f"{open_count:3d} open", "yellow")
   275→            if open_count > 0
   276→            else colorize_fn("  ✓", "green")
   277→        )
   278→        print(
   279→            f"  {det_label} {bar} {pct:3d}%  {open_str}  {colorize_fn(f'/ {total}', 'dim')}"
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    print()
   283→
   284→
   285→__all__ = [
   286→    "dimension_bar",
   287→    "show_dimension_deltas",
   288→    "show_detector_progress",
   289→    "show_low_dimension_hints",
   290→    "show_score_model_breakdown",
   291→]
   292→
```

```
    55→
    56→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    57→    """Print the scan header line."""
    58→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    62→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    63→    lines = [
    64→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    65→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    66→    ]
    67→    print("\n".join(lines))
    68→
    69→
    70→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    71→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    72→    if not effective_include_slow:
    73→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    74→    if noise.budget_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    76→    if noise.hidden_total:
    77→        print(
    78→            colorize(
    79→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    80→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    81→                "dim",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→
    85→
    86→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    87→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    88→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    89→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    for entry in warnings:
    93→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    94→            continue
    95→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    96→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    97→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    98→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
    99→
   100→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   101→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   102→        if impact:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   104→        if remediation:
   105→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   106→
   107→
   108→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   110→    try:
   111→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   112→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   113→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   114→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   115→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   116→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   117→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   118→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   119→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   120→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   121→        runtime,
   122→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   123→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   124→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   125→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   126→    )
   127→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   128→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   129→        print(
   130→            colorize(
   131→                "  * Subjective reset "
   132→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   133→                "yellow",
   134→            )
   135→        )
   136→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   140→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   141→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   142→                "yellow",
   143→            )
   144→        )
   145→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   146→
   147→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   148→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   149→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   150→
   151→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   152→
   153→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   154→    show_score_delta(
   155→        runtime.state,
   156→        merge.prev_overall,
   157→        merge.prev_objective,
   158→        merge.prev_strict,
   159→        merge.prev_verified,
   160→    )
   161→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   162→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   163→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   164→
   165→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   166→
   167→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   168→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   169→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   170→
   171→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   172→        merge.diff,
   173→        runtime.state,
   174→        runtime.lang,
   175→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   176→    )
   177→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   178→
   179→    write_query(
   180→        build_scan_query_payload(
   181→            runtime.state,
   182→            runtime.config,
   183→            runtime.profile,
   184→            merge.diff,
   185→            warnings,
   186→            narrative,
   187→            merge,
   188→            noise,
   189→        ),
   190→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   191→    )
   192→
   193→    badge_emit = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   194→    if isinstance(badge_emit, tuple):
   195→        badge_path, _badge_result = badge_emit
   196→    else:  # Backward-compatible shape for monkeypatched tests.
   197→        badge_path = badge_emit
   198→    _print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   199→    auto_update_skill()
   200→
   201→
   202→__all__ = [
   203→    "cmd_scan",
   204→]
   205→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan narrative and integrity reporting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
     7→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def _coerce_coverage_confidence(value: object, *, default: float = 1.0) -> float:
    12→    try:
    13→        parsed = float(value)
    14→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    15→        return default
    16→    return max(0.0, min(1.0, parsed))
    17→
    18→
    19→def _current_scan_coverage(state: dict, lang) -> dict:
    20→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
    21→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict):
    22→        return {}
    23→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) if lang is not None else None
    24→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
    25→        entry = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
    26→        return entry if isinstance(entry, dict) else {}
    27→    return {}
    28→
    29→
    30→def _coverage_reduction_warnings(state: dict, lang) -> list[str]:
    31→    coverage = _current_scan_coverage(state, lang)
    32→    detectors = coverage.get("detectors", {})
    33→    if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
    34→        return []
    35→
    36→    warnings: list[str] = []
    37→    for detector, payload in detectors.items():
    38→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    39→            continue
    40→        status = str(payload.get("status", "full")).strip().lower()
    41→        confidence = _coerce_coverage_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
    42→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
    43→            continue
    44→
    45→        summary = str(payload.get("summary", "")).strip()
    46→        impact = str(payload.get("impact", "")).strip()
    47→        remediation = str(payload.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    48→        detector_label = str(detector).strip() or "detector"
    49→
    50→        line = f"Coverage reduced ({detector_label}): {summary or 'reduced detector confidence.'}"
    51→        if impact:
    52→            line += f" Repercussion: {impact}"
    53→        if remediation:
    54→            line += f" Fix: {remediation}"
    55→        warnings.append(line)
    56→    return warnings
    57→
    58→
    59→def show_post_scan_analysis(
    60→    diff: dict,
    61→    state: dict,
    62→    lang,
    63→    *,
    64→    target_strict_score: float = 95.0,
    65→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
    66→    """Print critical warnings + headline + pointers. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
    67→    # Critical warnings only (reopened, cascading, chronic, coverage reduction)
    68→    warnings: list[str] = []
    69→    if diff["reopened"] > 5:
    70→        warnings.append(
    71→            f"{diff['reopened']} findings reopened — was a previous fix reverted?"
    72→        )
    73→    if diff["new"] > 10 and diff["auto_resolved"] < 3:
    74→        warnings.append(
    75→            f"{diff['new']} new findings with few resolutions — likely cascading."
    76→        )
    77→    chronic = diff.get("chronic_reopeners", [])
    78→    chronic_count = len(chronic) if isinstance(chronic, list) else chronic
    79→    if chronic_count > 0:
    80→        warnings.append(
    81→            f"⟳ {chronic_count} chronic reopener{'s' if chronic_count != 1 else ''} — "
    82→            "run `desloppify show --chronic` to see them."
    83→        )
    84→    warnings.extend(_coverage_reduction_warnings(state, lang))
    85→
    86→    for warning in warnings:
    87→        print(colorize(f"  {warning}", "yellow"))
    88→    if warnings:
    89→        print()
    90→
    91→    # Load plan for narrative cluster awareness
    92→    _plan_data = None
    93→    _plan = load_plan()
    94→    if _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters"):
    95→        _plan_data = _plan
    96→
    97→    # Single narrative headline
    98→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    99→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   100→        state,
   101→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(
   102→            diff=diff,
   103→            lang=lang_name,
   104→            command="scan",
   105→            plan=_plan_data,
   106→        ),
   107→    )
   108→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   109→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   110→
   111→    # Pointers — include plan reference when active
   112→    _has_plan = _plan_data is not None or has_living_plan()
   113→
   114→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` for the highest-priority item.", "dim"))
   115→    if _has_plan:
   116→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify plan` to see the updated living plan.", "dim"))
   117→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify status` for the full dashboard.", "dim"))
   118→    print()
   119→
   120→    return warnings, narrative
   121→
   122→
   123→def show_score_integrity(state: dict, diff: dict):
   124→    """Show Score Integrity section — surfaces wontfix debt and ignored findings."""
   125→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   126→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   127→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0)
   128→    ignore_patterns = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0)
   129→    score_confidence = state.get("score_confidence", {})
   130→    confidence_reduced = (
   131→        isinstance(score_confidence, dict)
   132→        and str(score_confidence.get("status", "full")).lower() == "reduced"
   133→    )
   134→
   135→    if wontfix <= 5 and ignored <= 0 and ignore_patterns <= 0 and not confidence_reduced:
   136→        return
   137→
   138→    overall = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   139→    strict = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   140→    strict_gap = (
   141→        round(overall - strict, 1) if overall is not None and strict is not None else 0
   142→    )
   143→
   144→    # Wontfix % of actionable findings (open + wontfix + fixed + auto_resolved + false_positive)
   145→    actionable = (
   146→        stats.get("open", 0)
   147→        + wontfix
   148→        + stats.get("fixed", 0)
   149→        + stats.get("auto_resolved", 0)
   150→        + stats.get("false_positive", 0)
   151→    )
   152→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
   153→
   154→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 2 + " Score Integrity " + "┄" * 37, "dim"))
   155→
   156→    if wontfix > 5:
   157→        if wontfix_pct > 50:
   158→            style = "red"
   159→            msg = (
   160→                f"  ❌ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — over half of findings swept under rug. "
   161→                f"Strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   162→            )
   163→        elif wontfix_pct > 25:
   164→            style = "yellow"
   165→            msg = (
   166→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — review whether past "
   167→                "wontfix decisions still hold"
   168→            )
   169→        elif wontfix_pct > 10:
   170→            style = "yellow"
   171→            msg = (
   172→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix findings ({wontfix_pct}%) — strict {strict_gap} "
   173→                "pts below lenient"
   174→            )
   175→        else:
   176→            style = "dim"
   177→            msg = f"  {wontfix} wontfix — strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   178→        print(colorize(msg, style))
   179→
   180→        # Show top 2 dimensions with biggest strict gap
   181→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   182→        if dim_scores:
   183→            gaps = []
   184→            for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   185→                score = data.get("score", 100)
   186→                strict_value = data.get("strict", score)
   187→                gap = round(score - strict_value, 1)
   188→                if gap > 0:
   189→                    gaps.append((name, gap))
   190→            gaps.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
   191→            if gaps:
   192→                top = gaps[:2]
   193→                gap_str = ", ".join(f"{name} (−{gap} pts)" for name, gap in top)
   194→                print(colorize(f"    Biggest gaps: {gap_str}", "dim"))
   195→
   196→    if ignored > 0:
   197→        style = "red" if ignore_patterns > 5 or ignored > 100 else "yellow"
   198→        print(
   199→            colorize(
   200→                f"  ⚠ {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   201→                f"suppressed {ignored} finding{'s' if ignored != 1 else ''} this scan",
   202→                style,
   203→            )
   204→        )
   205→        print(
   206→            colorize(
   207→                "    Suppressed findings still count against strict and verified scores",
   208→                "dim",
   209→            )
   210→        )
   211→    elif ignore_patterns > 0:
   212→        print(
   213→            colorize(
   214→                f"  {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   215→                "active (0 findings suppressed this scan)",
   216→                "dim",
   217→            )
   218→        )
   219→
   220→    if confidence_reduced:
   221→        impacted_dimensions = score_confidence.get("dimensions", [])
   222→        detectors = score_confidence.get("detectors", [])
   223→        confidence = _coerce_coverage_confidence(
   224→            score_confidence.get("confidence"),
   225→            default=1.0,
   226→        )
   227→        dim_text = ""
   228→        if isinstance(impacted_dimensions, list) and impacted_dimensions:
   229→            preview = ", ".join(str(item) for item in impacted_dimensions[:3])
   230→            if len(impacted_dimensions) > 3:
   231→                preview += f", +{len(impacted_dimensions) - 3} more"
   232→            dim_text = f" (dimensions: {preview})"
   233→        print(
   234→            colorize(
   235→                f"  ⚠ Score confidence reduced to {confidence * 100:.0f}%{dim_text}",
   236→                "yellow",
   237→            )
   238→        )
   239→        if isinstance(detectors, list):
   240→            for detector in detectors[:3]:
   241→                if not isinstance(detector, dict):
   242→                    continue
   243→                summary = str(detector.get("summary", "")).strip()
   244→                remediation = str(detector.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   245→                if summary:
   246→                    print(colorize(f"    - {summary}", "dim"))
   247→                if remediation:
   248→                    print(colorize(f"      Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   249→
   250→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 55, "dim"))
   251→    print()
   252→
   253→
   254→__all__ = ["show_post_scan_analysis", "show_score_integrity"]  # show_score_integrity used by status
   255→
```

```
   343→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   344→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   345→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   346→        return
   347→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   348→    if not explain:
   349→        return
   350→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   351→    if available:
   352→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   353→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   354→
   355→
   356→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   357→    if queue.get("items"):
   358→        return False
   359→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   360→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   361→    if tier is not None:
   362→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   363→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   364→        if available:
   365→            commands = " | ".join(
   366→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   367→            )
   368→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   369→    return True
   370→

   372→def render_terminal_items(
   373→    items: list[dict],
   374→    dim_scores: dict,
   375→    findings_scoped: dict,
   376→    *,
   377→    group: str,
   378→    explain: bool,
   379→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   380→    plan: dict | None = None,
   381→) -> None:
   382→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   383→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   384→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   385→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   386→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   387→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   388→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   389→
   390→    if group != "item":
   391→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   392→        return
   393→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   394→        if idx > 0:
   395→            print()
   396→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   397→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   398→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   399→        elif len(items) > 1:
   400→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   401→        else:
   402→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   403→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   404→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   405→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   406→
   407→
   408→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   409→    if len(items) != 1:
   410→        return
   411→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   412→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   413→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   414→        return
   415→    item = items[0]
   416→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   417→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   418→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   419→        primary = item.get(
   420→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   421→        )
   422→        print(f"    {primary}")
   423→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   424→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   425→        return
   426→
   427→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   428→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   429→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   430→        print(f"    {primary}")
   431→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   432→    else:
   433→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   434→
   435→    print(
   436→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   437→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   438→    )
   439→    print(
   440→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   441→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   442→    )

   444→
   445→def render_followup_nudges(
   446→    state: dict,
   447→    dim_scores: dict,
   448→    findings_scoped: dict,
   449→    *,
   450→    strict_score: float | None,
   451→    target_strict_score: float,
   452→) -> None:
   453→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   454→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   455→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   456→        state,
   457→        subjective_entries,
   458→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   459→        max_quality_items=3,
   460→        max_integrity_items=5,
   461→    )
   462→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   463→    if strict_score is not None:
   464→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   465→        if gap > 0:
   466→            print(
   467→                colorize(
   468→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   469→                    "cyan",
   470→                )
   471→            )
   472→        else:
   473→            print(
   474→                colorize(
   475→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   476→                    "green",
   477→                )
   478→            )
   479→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   480→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   481→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   482→
   483→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   484→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   485→        findings_scoped
   486→    )
   487→    parts: list[str] = []
   488→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   489→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   490→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   491→    open_review = [
   492→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   493→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   494→    ]
   495→    if low_dims:
   496→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   497→    if stale_count:
   498→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   499→    if unassessed_count:
   500→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   501→    if len(open_review):
   502→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   503→    if coverage_open > 0:
   504→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   505→
   506→    if parts:
   507→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   508→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   509→
   510→
   511→__all__ = [
   512→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   513→    "render_followup_nudges",
   514→    "render_queue_header",
   515→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   516→    "render_terminal_items",
   517→    "scorecard_subjective",
   518→    "show_empty_queue",
   519→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   520→]
   521→
```

```
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    76→    if config_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→
    79→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    80→
    81→
    82→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    83→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    84→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    85→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    86→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    87→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    88→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    89→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    90→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    91→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    92→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    93→
    94→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    95→
    96→    # Load the living plan
    97→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    98→    plan = load_plan()
    99→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   100→    if (
   101→        plan.get("queue_order")
   102→        or plan.get("overrides")
   103→        or plan.get("clusters")
   104→    ):
   105→        plan_data = plan
   106→
   107→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   109→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   110→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   111→        if active_cluster:
   112→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   113→
   114→    queue = build_work_queue(
   115→        state,
   116→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   117→            tier=tier,
   118→            count=count,
   119→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   120→            scope=scope,
   121→            status=status,
   122→            include_subjective=True,
   123→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   124→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   125→            explain=explain,
   126→            plan=plan_data,
   127→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   128→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   129→        ),
   130→    )
   131→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   132→
   133→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   134→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   135→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   136→        state,
   137→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   141→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   142→    )
   143→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   144→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   145→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   147→        state,
   148→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   149→    )
   150→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   151→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   152→    ]
   153→    write_query(payload)
   154→
   155→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   156→    if output_file:
   157→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   158→            output_file,
   159→            payload,
   160→            len(items),
   161→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   162→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   163→        ):
   164→            return
   165→        raise SystemExit(1)
   166→
   167→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   171→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   172→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   176→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   177→        state.get("findings", {}),
   178→        state.get("scan_path"),
   179→    )
   180→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   183→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   184→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   186→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   187→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   188→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   189→    )
   190→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   191→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(

   194→        findings_scoped,
   195→        strict_score=strict_score,
   196→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   197→    )
   198→    print()
   199→
   200→
   201→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
```

```
    42→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    43→    """Show score dashboard."""
    44→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    45→    state = runtime.state
    46→    config = runtime.config
    47→
    48→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    49→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    50→    scorecard_dims = scorecard_dimensions_payload(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    51→    subjective_measures = [row for row in scorecard_dims if row.get("subjective")]
    52→    suppression = state_mod.suppression_metrics(state)
    53→
    54→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    55→        print(
    56→            json.dumps(
    57→                _status_json_payload(
    58→                    state,
    59→                    stats,
    60→                    dim_scores,
    61→                    scorecard_dims,
    62→                    subjective_measures,
    63→                    suppression,
    64→                ),
    65→                indent=2,
    66→            )
    67→        )
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    71→        return
    72→
    73→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    74→    if stale_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    76→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    77→    if skill_warning:
    78→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    79→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    80→    if config_warning:
    81→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    82→
    83→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    84→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    85→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    86→
    87→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    88→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    89→
    90→    # Load living plan for plan-aware rendering and narrative
    91→    _plan = load_plan()
    92→    _plan_active = _plan if (
    93→        _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ) else None
    95→
    96→    narrative = compute_narrative(
    97→        state,
    98→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="status", plan=_plan_active),
    99→    )
   100→    ignores = config.get("ignore", [])
   101→
   102→    for line, style in score_summary_lines(
   103→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   104→        objective_score=scores.objective,
   105→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   106→        verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   107→    ):
   108→        print(colorize(line, style))
   109→    print_scan_metrics(state)
   110→    print_open_scope_breakdown(state)
   111→    print_scan_completeness(state)
   112→
   113→    if dim_scores:
   114→        show_dimension_table(state, dim_scores)
   115→        reporting_dimensions_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(
   116→            state,
   117→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
   118→        )
   119→    else:
   120→        show_tier_progress_table(by_tier)
   121→
   122→    if dim_scores:
   123→        show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores, state, plan=_plan_active)
   124→        show_subjective_followup(
   125→            state,
   126→            dim_scores,
   127→            target_strict_score=target_strict_score,
   128→        )
   129→
   130→    show_review_summary(state)
   131→    show_structural_areas(state)
   132→    show_agent_plan(narrative, plan=_plan_active)
   133→
   134→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   135→        print(colorize(f"  -> {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   136→        print()
   137→
   138→    if ignores:
   139→        show_ignore_summary(ignores, suppression)
   140→
   141→    review_age = config.get("review_max_age_days", 30)
   142→    if review_age != 30:
   143→        label = "never" if review_age == 0 else f"{review_age} days"
   144→        print(colorize(f"  Review staleness: {label}", "dim"))
   145→    print()
   146→
   147→    write_status_query(
   148→        state=state,
   149→        stats=stats,
   150→        by_tier=by_tier,
   151→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   152→        scorecard_dims=scorecard_dims,
   153→        subjective_measures=subjective_measures,
   154→        suppression=suppression,
   155→        narrative=narrative,
   156→        ignores=ignores,
   157→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   158→        objective_score=scores.objective,
   159→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   160→        verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   161→        plan=_plan_active,
   162→    )

   165→def _status_json_payload(
   166→    state: dict,
   167→    stats: dict,
   168→    dim_scores: dict,
   169→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
   170→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
   171→    suppression: dict,
   172→) -> dict:
   173→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   174→    findings = state.get("findings", {})

   176→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   177→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
   178→        else None
   179→    )
   180→    return {
   181→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
   182→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
   183→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
   184→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,

   186→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,

   190→        "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   191→        "stats": stats,
   192→        "open_scope": open_scope,
   193→        "suppression": suppression,
   194→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   195→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   196→    }
   197→
```

```
    32→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    33→
    34→
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def _status_next_command(narrative: dict) -> str:
    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"
    71→
    72→
    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    stats: dict,
    77→    by_tier: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
    80→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
    81→    suppression: dict,
    82→    narrative: dict,
    83→    ignores: list[str],
    84→    overall_score: float | None,
    85→    objective_score: float | None,
    86→    strict_score: float | None,
    87→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    88→    plan: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> None:
    90→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    91→    open_scope = (
    92→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    93→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    94→        else None
    95→    )
    96→    write_query(
    97→        {
    98→            "command": "status",
    99→            "overall_score": overall_score,
   100→            "objective_score": objective_score,
   101→            "strict_score": strict_score,
   102→            "verified_strict_score": verified_strict_score,
   103→            "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   104→            "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   105→            "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   106→            "stats": stats,
   107→            "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   108→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   109→            "by_tier": by_tier,

   111→            "suppression": suppression,
   112→            "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   113→            "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   114→            "open_scope": open_scope,
   115→            "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   116→            "next_command": _status_next_command(narrative),
   117→            "narrative": narrative,
   118→            **({"plan": {
   119→                "active": True,
   120→                "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   121→                "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   122→                "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   123→                "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   124→            }} if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters") or plan.get("skipped")) else {}),
   125→        }
   126→    )

   129→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Show ignore list plus suppression accountability from recent scans."""
   131→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))
   132→    for p in ignores[:10]:
   133→        print(colorize(f"    {p}", "dim"))
   134→
   135→    last_ignored = int(suppression.get("last_ignored", 0) or 0)
   136→    last_raw = int(suppression.get("last_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   137→    last_pct = float(suppression.get("last_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   138→
   139→    if last_raw > 0:
   140→        style = "red" if last_pct >= 30 else "yellow" if last_pct >= 10 else "dim"
   141→        print(
   142→            colorize(
   143→                f"  Ignore suppression (last scan): {last_ignored}/{last_raw} findings hidden ({last_pct:.1f}%)",
   144→                style,
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    elif suppression.get("recent_scans", 0):
   148→        print(colorize("  Ignore suppression (last scan): 0 findings hidden", "dim"))
   149→
   150→    recent_scans = int(suppression.get("recent_scans", 0) or 0)
   151→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   152→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   153→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   154→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   155→        print(
   156→            colorize(
   157→                f"    Recent ({recent_scans} scans): {recent_ignored}/{recent_raw} findings hidden ({recent_pct:.1f}%)",
   158→                "dim",
   159→            )
   160→        )
```
