   163→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   164→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   165→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   166→        state,
   167→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   168→    )
   169→
   170→
   171→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   172→    dim_scores: dict,
   173→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   174→) -> str | None:
   175→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   176→    lowest_name = None
   177→    lowest_score = 101.0
   178→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   179→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   180→        if not ds:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   183→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   184→            lowest_score = strict_val
   185→            lowest_name = dim.name
   186→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   187→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   188→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   189→            lowest_score = strict_val
   190→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   191→    return lowest_name
   192→
   193→
   194→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   195→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   196→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   197→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   198→        return {}
   199→
   200→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for finding in findings.values():
   202→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   203→            continue
   204→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   205→            continue
   206→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   207→        dimension = ""
   208→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   209→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   210→        if not dimension:
   211→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   212→        if not dimension:
   213→            continue
   214→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   215→    return counts
   216→
   217→
   218→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   219→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   222→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   223→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   224→        if not ds:
   225→            continue
   226→        score_val = ds["score"]
   227→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   228→        checks = ds["checks"]
   229→
   230→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   231→
   232→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   233→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   234→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   235→        print(
   236→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   237→        )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   241→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   242→    *,
   243→    lowest_name: str | None,
   244→    bar_len: int,
   245→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   248→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   249→        return
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   253→            "dim",
   254→        )
   255→    )
   256→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   257→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   258→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   259→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   260→        tier = 4
   261→
   262→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   263→
   264→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   265→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   266→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   267→        placeholder_tag = (
   268→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   269→        )
   270→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   271→        cli_keys = [
   272→            str(key).strip()
   273→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   274→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   275→        ]
   276→        if dim_key:
   277→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   278→        elif cli_keys:
   279→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   280→        else:
   281→            issue_count = 0
   282→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   283→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   284→        print(
   285→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   286→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   287→        )
   288→
   289→
   290→def _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective: list[dict], state: dict | None = None) -> None:
   291→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   292→    print(
   293→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   294→    )
   295→    print(
   296→        colorize(
   297→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
   298→            "dim",
   299→        )
   300→    )
   301→    stale_keys = [
   302→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   303→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   304→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   305→    ]
   306→    if stale_keys:
   307→        n = len(stale_keys)
   308→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   309→        print(
   310→            colorize(
   311→                f"  [stale] = subjective assessment outdated, not an unresolved finding"
   312→                f" — re-review to refresh",
   313→                "yellow",
   314→            )
   315→        )
   316→        print(
   317→            colorize(
   318→                f"  {n} stale dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   319→                f": `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}`",
   320→                "yellow",
   321→            )
   322→        )
   323→
   324→
   325→def show_dimension_table(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   326→    """Show dimension health table with dual scores and progress bars."""
   327→    print()
   328→    bar_len = 20
   329→    print(
   330→        colorize(
   331→            f"  {'Dimension':<22} {'Checks':>7}  {'Health':>6}  {'Strict':>6}  {'Bar':<{bar_len + 2}} {'Tier'}  {'Action'}",
   332→            "dim",
   333→        )
   334→    )
   335→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 86, "dim"))
   336→
   337→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   338→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   339→    review_issue_counts = _open_review_issue_counts(state)
   340→
   341→    _render_objective_dimensions(dim_scores, lowest_name=lowest_name, bar_len=bar_len)
   342→    _render_subjective_dimensions(
   343→        scorecard_subjective,
   344→        lowest_name=lowest_name,
   345→        bar_len=bar_len,
   346→        review_issue_counts=review_issue_counts,
   347→    )
   348→    _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective, state=state)
   349→    print()

   351→
   352→def show_focus_suggestion(
   353→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   354→) -> None:
   355→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   356→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   357→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   358→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   359→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   360→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   361→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   362→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   363→        print(
   364→            colorize(
   365→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   366→                "cyan",
   367→            )
   368→        )
   369→        print()
   370→        return
   371→
   372→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   373→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   374→    if not lowest_name:
   375→        return
   376→
   377→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   378→    lowest_kind = None
   379→    lowest_score = 101.0
   380→    lowest_issues = 0
   381→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   382→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   383→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   384→            if ds:
   385→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   386→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   387→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   388→            break
   389→    else:
   390→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   391→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   392→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   394→                lowest_issues = 0
   395→                break
   396→
   397→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   398→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   399→            print(
   400→                colorize(
   401→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   402→                    "cyan",
   403→                )
   404→            )
   405→            print()
   406→            return
   407→
   408→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   409→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   410→        if target_dim:
   411→            impact = 0.0
   412→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   413→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   414→                    {
   415→                        k: {
   416→                            "score": v["score"],
   417→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   418→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   419→                        }
   420→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   421→                        if "score" in v
   422→                    },
   423→                    potentials,
   424→                    det,
   425→                    lowest_issues,
   426→                )
   427→                if impact > 0:
   428→                    break
   429→
   430→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   431→            print(
   432→                colorize(
   433→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   434→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   435→                    "cyan",
   436→                )
   437→            )
   438→            print()
   439→

   443→) -> None:

   493→def _collect_structural_areas(
   494→    state: dict,
   495→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   496→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   497→
   498→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   499→    """
   500→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   501→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   502→    )
   503→
   504→    structural = [
   505→        f
   506→        for f in findings.values()
   507→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   508→    ]
   509→
   510→    if len(structural) < 5:
   511→        return None
   512→
   513→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   514→    for f in structural:
   515→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   516→        areas[area].append(f)
   517→
   518→    if len(areas) < 2:
   519→        return None
   520→
   521→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   522→
   523→
   524→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   525→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   526→    rows = []
   527→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   528→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   529→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   530→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   531→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   532→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   533→        rows.append(
   534→            [
   535→                area,
   536→                str(len(area_findings)),
   537→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   538→                str(open_count),
   539→                str(debt_count),
   540→                str(weight),
   541→            ]
   542→        )
   543→    return rows
   544→
   545→
   546→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   547→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   548→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   549→    if remaining > 0:
   550→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   551→
   552→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   553→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   554→    print(
   555→        colorize(
   556→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   557→        )
   558→    )
   559→    print(
   560→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   561→    )
   562→    print()
   563→
   564→
   565→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   566→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   567→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   568→    if sorted_areas is None:
   569→        return
   570→
   571→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   572→    print(
   573→        colorize(
   574→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   575→            "dim",
   576→        )
   577→    )
   578→    print()
   579→
   580→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   581→    print_table(
   582→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   583→    )
   584→
   585→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   586→
   587→
   588→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   589→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   590→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   591→    review_open = [
   592→        f
   593→        for f in findings.values()
   594→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   595→    ]
   596→    if not review_open:
   597→        return
   598→    uninvestigated = sum(
   599→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   600→    )
   601→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   602→    if uninvestigated:
   603→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   604→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   605→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   606→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   607→        print(
   608→            colorize(
   609→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   610→                "dim",
   611→            )
   612→        )
   613→    print()
   614→

     2→

     8→
     9→def score_summary_lines(
    10→    *,
    11→    overall_score: float | None,
    12→    objective_score: float | None,
    13→    strict_score: float | None,
    14→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    15→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    16→    """Return formatted top-line score summary rows."""

    18→        overall_score is not None
    19→        and objective_score is not None
    20→        and strict_score is not None
    21→        and verified_strict_score is not None
    22→    ):

    24→            (
    25→                f"\n  Scores: overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 · "

    34→                "dim",
    35→            ),
    36→            ("  Focus on strict — it's your north star.", "dim"),
    37→        ]
    38→    return [
    39→        ("\n  Scores unavailable", "bold"),
    40→        ("  Run a full scan to compute overall/objective/strict scores.", "yellow"),
    41→    ]
    42→
    43→
    44→def print_scan_metrics(state: dict) -> None:
    45→    """Print aggregate codebase metrics from the last scan."""
    46→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    47→    total_files = sum(m.get("total_files", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    48→    total_loc = sum(m.get("total_loc", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    49→    total_dirs = sum(m.get("total_directories", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    50→    if total_files:
    51→        loc_str = f"{total_loc:,}" if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(
    54→                f"  {total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} dirs · "
    55→                f"Last scan: {state.get('last_scan', 'never')}",
    56→                "dim",
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→        return
    60→    print(
    61→        colorize(
    62→            f"  Scans: {state.get('scan_count', 0)} | Last: {state.get('last_scan', 'never')}",
    63→            "dim",
    64→        )
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def print_scan_completeness(state: dict) -> None:
    69→    """Warn when one or more language scans were partial."""
    70→    completeness = state.get("scan_completeness", {})
    71→    incomplete = [lang for lang, status in completeness.items() if status != "full"]
    72→    if incomplete:
    73→        print(
    74→            colorize(
    75→                f"  * Incomplete scan ({', '.join(incomplete)} — slow phases skipped)",
    76→                "yellow",
    77→            )
    78→        )
    79→
    80→
    81→def print_open_scope_breakdown(state: dict) -> None:
    82→    """Print open counts with explicit in-scope/out-of-scope semantics."""
    83→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    84→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
    85→        return
    86→
    87→    counts = state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    88→    print(
    89→        colorize(
    90→            "  "
    91→            f"open (in-scope): {counts['in_scope']} · "
    92→            f"open (out-of-scope carried): {counts['out_of_scope']} · "
    93→            f"open (global): {counts['global']}",
    94→            "dim",
    95→        )
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→__all__ = [
   100→    "print_open_scope_breakdown",
   101→    "print_scan_completeness",
   102→    "print_scan_metrics",
   103→    "score_summary_lines",

    29→def _detect(fixer: FixerConfig, path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    30→    print(colorize(f"\nDetecting {fixer.label}...", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→    entries = fixer.detect(path)
    32→    file_count = len(set(e["file"] for e in entries))
    33→    print(
    34→        colorize(
    35→            f"  Found {len(entries)} {fixer.label} across {file_count} files\n", "dim"
    36→        ),
    37→        file=sys.stderr,
    38→    )
    39→    return entries
    40→
    41→
    42→def _print_fix_summary(
    43→    fixer: FixerConfig,
    44→    results: list[dict],
    45→    total_items: int,
    46→    total_lines: int,
    47→    dry_run: bool,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    verb = fixer.dry_verb if dry_run else fixer.verb
    50→    lines_str = f" ({total_lines} lines)" if total_lines else ""
    51→    print(
    52→        colorize(
    53→            f"\n  {verb} {total_items} {fixer.label} across {len(results)} files{lines_str}\n",
    54→            "bold",
    55→        )
    56→    )
    57→    for r in results[:30]:
    58→        syms = ", ".join(r["removed"][:5])
    59→        if len(r["removed"]) > 5:
    60→            syms += f" (+{len(r['removed']) - 5})"
    61→        extra = f"  ({r['lines_removed']} lines)" if r.get("lines_removed") else ""
    62→        print(f"  {rel(r['file'])}{extra}  →  {syms}")
    63→    if len(results) > 30:
    64→        print(f"  ... and {len(results) - 30} more files")
    65→
    66→
    67→def _apply_and_report(
    68→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    69→    path: Path,
    70→    fixer: FixerConfig,
    71→    fixer_name: str,
    72→    entries: list[dict],
    73→    results: list[dict],
    74→    total_items: int,
    75→    lang: LangRun | None,
    76→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] | None = None,
    77→) -> None:
    78→    state_file = state_path(args)
    79→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    80→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    81→    resolved_ids = _resolve_fixer_results(state, results, fixer.detector, fixer_name)
    82→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    83→
    84→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    85→    print(f"\n  Auto-resolved {len(resolved_ids)} findings in state")
    86→    print_score_update(state, prev)
    87→
    88→    if fixer.post_fix:
    89→        fixer.post_fix(path, state, prev.overall or 0, False, lang=lang)
    90→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    91→
    92→    if skip_reasons is None:
    93→        skip_reasons = {}
    94→    fix_lang = resolve_lang(args)
    95→    fix_lang_name = fix_lang.name if fix_lang else None
    96→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    97→        state,
    98→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=fix_lang_name, command="fix"),
    99→    )
   100→    typecheck_cmd = getattr(lang, "typecheck_cmd", "")

1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    12→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    13→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    14→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    15→        print(f"  {fid}")
    16→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    17→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    18→
    19→
    20→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    21→    state: dict,
    22→    *,
    23→    status: str,
    24→    resolved_count: int,
    25→) -> None:
    26→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    27→        return
    28→    wontfix_count = sum(
    29→        1 for finding in state["findings"].values() if finding["status"] == "wontfix"
    30→    )
    31→    actionable = sum(
    32→        1
    33→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    34→        if finding["status"]
    35→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    36→    )
    37→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    38→    print(
    39→        colorize(
    40→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    41→            "yellow",
    42→        )
    43→    )
    44→    print(
    45→        colorize(
    46→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    47→            "dim",
    48→        )
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    53→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    54→        return ""
    55→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    56→
    57→
    58→def _print_score_movement(
    59→    *,
    60→    status: str,
    61→    prev_overall: float | None,
    62→    prev_objective: float | None,
    63→    prev_strict: float | None,
    64→    prev_verified: float | None,
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    67→) -> None:
    68→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    69→    if (
    70→        new.overall is None
    71→        or new.objective is None
    72→        or new.strict is None
    73→        or new.verified is None
    74→    ):
    75→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    79→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    80→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    81→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    82→    print(
    83→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    84→        + colorize(
    85→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    89→        + colorize(
    90→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    91→        )
    92→    )
    93→    if status == "wontfix":
    94→        strict_gap = round(new.overall - new.strict, 1)
    95→        if strict_gap > 0:
    96→            print(
    97→                colorize(
    98→                    f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
    99→                    f"Current gap: overall {new.overall:.1f} vs strict {new.strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
   100→                    "yellow",
   101→                )
   102→            )
   103→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
   107→                "yellow",
   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→        print(
   111→            colorize(
   112→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
   113→                "dim",
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→    elif status == "fixed":
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→
   125→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   126→    *,
   127→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   128→    state: dict,
   129→    all_resolved: list[str],
   130→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   131→) -> None:
   132→    has_review = any(
   133→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   134→        for fid in all_resolved
   135→    )
   136→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    stale_dims = sorted(
   140→        dim
   141→        for dim in {
   142→            str(
   143→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   144→            ).strip()
   145→            for fid in all_resolved
   146→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   147→        }
   148→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   149→    )
   150→    if not stale_dims:
   151→        return
   152→
   153→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   154→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   155→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   156→    print(
   157→        colorize(
   158→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   159→            "yellow",
   160→        )
   161→    )
   162→    print(
   163→        colorize(
   164→            "  Next subjective step: "
   165→            + f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(stale_dims)}`",
   166→            "dim",
   167→        )
   168→    )
   169→
   170→
   171→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   172→    remaining = sum(
   173→        1
   174→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   175→        if finding["status"] == "open"
   176→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   177→    )
   178→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   179→    if remaining > 0:
   180→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   181→        print(
   182→            colorize(
   183→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   184→                "dim",
   185→            )
   186→        )
   187→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   188→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   189→    print()
   190→    return next_command
   191→

    51→MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 100
    52→_HIGH_IGNORE_SUPPRESSION_THRESHOLD = 30.0
    53→_WONTFIX_GAP_THRESHOLD = 1.0

    55→
    56→def _resolve_target_strict_score(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str | None]:
    57→    """Resolve strict-score target from config with bounded fallback."""
    58→    raw_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    59→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    60→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE)
    61→    try:
    62→        target = int(raw_target)
    63→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    64→        return (
    65→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    66→            (
    67→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    68→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    69→            ),
    70→        )
    71→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:
    72→        return (
    73→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    74→            (
    75→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    76→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    77→            ),
    78→        )
    79→    return target, None
    80→

    83→    """Build strict-target context for command rendering and agents."""

   110→
   111→def _count_open_by_detector(findings: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   112→    """Count open findings by detector, merging structural sub-detectors.
   113→
   114→    When detector is "review" and detail.holistic is True, also increments
   115→    "review_holistic" for separate holistic counting.
   116→    """
   117→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   118→    for f in findings.values():
   119→        if f["status"] != "open" or f.get("suppressed"):
   120→            continue
   121→        detector = f.get("detector", "unknown")
   122→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   123→            detector = "structural"
   124→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   125→        # Track holistic review findings separately
   126→        if detector == "review" and f.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
   127→            by_detector["review_holistic"] = by_detector.get("review_holistic", 0) + 1
   128→    # Track uninvestigated review findings (only when review findings exist)
   129→    if by_detector.get("review", 0) > 0:
   130→        by_detector["review_uninvestigated"] = sum(
   131→            1
   132→            for f in findings.values()
   133→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→            and not f.get("suppressed")
   135→            and f.get("detector") == "review"
   136→            and not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   137→        )
   138→    return by_detector
   139→
   140→
   141→def _resolve_badge_path(project_root: Path) -> tuple[str, Path]:
   142→    """Resolve badge path from config, defaulting to root-level scorecard.png."""
   143→    default_rel = "scorecard.png"
   144→    config = {}
   145→    try:
   146→        config = _load_config()
   147→    except (AttributeError, OSError):
   148→        config = {}
   149→
   150→    raw_path = default_rel
   151→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   152→        configured = config.get("badge_path")
   153→        if isinstance(configured, str) and configured.strip():
   154→            raw_path = configured.strip()
   155→
   156→    path = Path(raw_path)
   157→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   158→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   159→        return raw_path, project_root / path
   160→
   161→    try:
   162→        rel_path = str(path.relative_to(project_root))
   163→    except ValueError:
   164→        rel_path = str(path)
   165→    return rel_path, path
   166→
   167→
   168→def _compute_badge_status() -> BadgeStatus:
   169→    """Check configured scorecard path and whether README references it."""
   170→    project_root = PROJECT_ROOT
   171→
   172→    scorecard_rel, scorecard_path = _resolve_badge_path(project_root)
   173→    generated = scorecard_path.exists()
   174→
   175→    in_readme = False
   176→    if generated:
   177→        for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   178→            readme_path = project_root / readme_name
   179→            if readme_path.exists():
   180→                try:
   181→                    in_readme = scorecard_rel in readme_path.read_text(
   182→                        encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   183→                    )
   184→                except OSError:
   185→                    in_readme = False
   186→                break
   187→
   188→    recommendation = None
   189→    if generated and not in_readme:
   190→        recommendation = (
   191→            f'Add to README: <img src="{scorecard_rel}" width="100%">'
   192→        )
   193→
   194→    return {
   195→        "generated": generated,
   196→        "in_readme": in_readme,
   197→        "path": scorecard_rel,
   198→        "recommendation": recommendation,
   199→    }

   123→def _print_drag_summary(dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   124→    """Print the biggest score-drag dimensions so agents know where to focus."""

   131→            [e for e in entries if isinstance(e, dict) and float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0) > 0.01],
   132→            key=lambda e: -float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0),
   133→        )
   134→        if drags:
   135→            print("Biggest score drags (fixing these dimensions has the most impact):")
   136→            for entry in drags[:5]:
   137→                print(
   138→                    f"  - {entry['name']}: -{float(entry['overall_drag']):.2f} pts "
   139→                    f"(score {float(entry['score']):.1f}%, "
   140→                    f"{float(entry['pool_share'])*100:.1f}% of {entry['pool']} pool)"
   141→                )
   142→            print()
   143→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   144→        logger.debug("Drag summary skipped: %s", exc)

   147→def _print_stats_summary(

   207→def _detect_agent_interface() -> str | None:
   208→    """Detect the current agent interface from environment variables."""
   209→    if os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CODE"):
   210→        return "claude"
   211→    if os.environ.get("GEMINI_CLI"):
   212→        return "gemini"
   213→    if os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX_NETWORK_DISABLED") or os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX"):

   216→        return "cursor"

   220→def _try_auto_update_skill() -> None:
   221→    """Attempt to auto-install or auto-update the skill document.
   222→
   223→    Best-effort: swallows all exceptions so a network failure or permission
   224→    error never breaks the scan.
   225→    """
   226→    install = skill_docs_mod.find_installed_skill()
   227→
   228→    if install and not install.stale:
   229→        return  # Up to date.
   230→
   231→    try:
   232→        from desloppify.app.commands.update_skill import (
   233→            resolve_interface,
   234→            update_installed_skill,
   235→        )
   236→
   237→        if install:
   238→            interface = resolve_interface(install=install)
   239→        else:
   240→            interface = _detect_agent_interface()
   241→
   242→        if interface:
   243→            update_installed_skill(interface)
   244→    except (ImportError, OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
   245→        logger.debug("Skill auto-update skipped: %s", exc, exc_info=True)

   247→
   248→def _print_badge_hint(badge_path: Path | None) -> None:
   249→    if not (badge_path and badge_path.exists()):
   250→        return
   251→
   252→    rel_path = badge_path.name if badge_path.parent == PROJECT_ROOT else str(badge_path)
   253→    print(f"A scorecard image was saved to `{rel_path}`.")
   254→    print("Let the user know they can view it, and suggest adding it")
   255→    print(f'to their README: `<img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">`')
   256→
   257→
   258→def _print_llm_summary(
   259→    state: dict,
   260→    badge_path: Path | None,
   261→    narrative: dict | None = None,
   262→    diff: dict | None = None,
   263→):
   264→    """Print a structured summary for LLM consumption.
   265→
   266→    The LLM reads terminal output after running scans. This gives it
   267→    clear instructions on how to present the results to the end user.
   268→    Only shown when running inside an agent (CLAUDE_CODE or DESLOPPIFY_AGENT env).
   269→    """
   270→    if not _is_agent_environment():
   271→        return
   272→
   273→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   274→    scores = _load_scores(state)
   275→
   276→    if (
   277→        scores.overall is None
   278→        and scores.objective is None
   279→        and scores.strict is None
   280→        and scores.verified is None
   281→        and not dim_scores
   282→    ):
   283→        return
   284→
   285→    print("─" * 60)
   286→    print("INSTRUCTIONS FOR LLM")
   287→    print("IMPORTANT: ALWAYS present ALL scores to the user after a scan.")
   288→    print("Show overall health (lenient + strict), ALL dimension scores,")
   289→    print("AND all subjective dimension scores in a markdown table.")
   290→    print("The goal is to maximize strict scores. Never skip the scores.\n")
   291→
   292→    # Check for living plan
   293→    try:
   294→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   295→        _plan = load_plan()
   296→        _has_plan = bool(
   297→            _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   298→            or _plan.get("skipped")
   299→        )
   300→    except Exception:
   301→        _plan = {}
   302→        _has_plan = False
   303→
   304→    if _has_plan:
   305→        ordered = len(_plan.get("queue_order", []))
   306→        skipped = len(_plan.get("skipped", {}))
   307→        active = _plan.get("active_cluster")
   308→        print(f"LIVING PLAN ACTIVE: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.")
   309→        if active:
   310→            cluster = _plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   311→            remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   312→            print(f"Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).")
   313→        print("The plan is the single source of truth for work order.")
   314→        print("Use `desloppify next` which respects the plan.")
   315→        print("Use `desloppify plan` to view and update it.\n")
   316→
   317→    _print_score_lines(
   318→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   319→        objective_score=scores.objective,
   320→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   321→        verified_score=scores.verified,
   322→    )
   323→    _print_dimension_table(state, dim_scores)
   324→    _print_drag_summary(dim_scores)
   325→    _print_stats_summary(
   326→        state,
   327→        diff,
   328→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   329→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   330→    )
   331→    if _has_plan:
   332→        print("\nFollow the living plan: `desloppify next` for your next task,")
   333→        print("`desloppify plan` to view the full queue.")
   334→    else:
   335→        _print_workflow_guide()
   336→    _print_narrative_status(narrative)
   337→    _print_badge_hint(badge_path)
   338→    print("─" * 60)
   339→
   340→
   341→def auto_update_skill() -> None:
   342→    """Auto-install or update the skill document if we detect an agent.
   343→
   344→    Called unconditionally from the scan workflow — not gated on scores.
   345→    """
   346→    if not _is_agent_environment():
   347→        return
   348→
   349→    _try_auto_update_skill()
   350→
   351→    # Single post-check: whatever happened above, is the doc current now?
   352→    install = skill_docs_mod.find_installed_skill()
   353→    if not install:
   354→        names = ", ".join(sorted(skill_docs_mod.SKILL_TARGETS))

   359→    elif install.stale:

   361→            f"Skill document is outdated "
   362→            f"(v{install.version}, current v{skill_docs_mod.SKILL_VERSION}). "
   363→            f"Run: desloppify update-skill"

## Score Display Locations

### 1. `scan` command — the primary score display

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan.py` (line 154-176)

The scan command calls two score-display functions in sequence:

Prints the canonical four-score line:
```

```
with delta suffixes like `(+5.0)` or `(-2.0)`. Also prints a score legend on first scan or when the strict gap is large. Does NOT print any target score.

**`show_post_scan_analysis()`** — defined in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_analysis.py` (line 59-120)

Prints the narrative headline, then three pointers:
```
  Run `desloppify next` for the highest-priority item.
  Run `desloppify plan` to see the updated living plan.
  Run `desloppify status` for the full dashboard.
```
No target score printed here.

The `scan.py` code computes `target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(...)` at line 165 and passes it to `show_post_scan_analysis` as `target_strict_score=target_value`, but that parameter is only used inside `show_post_scan_analysis` to pass along to the narrative context — it is **never displayed** to the terminal from this path.

### 2. `status` command

```
No target score displayed here.

`show_subjective_followup()` uses `target_strict_score` but only to threshold which subjective dimensions show as "below target" — it doesn't print the target value directly.

### 3. `next` command

with color-coded deltas. When `config` is passed in, it also calls `format_strict_target_progress()` to print the strict target progress line.

`_print_next_command()` (line 171-190) — prints "N findings remaining — run `desloppify next`" and "Next command: `desloppify next`" (or `desloppify scan`).

### 6. Strict target progress — the dedicated target display helper

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_parts/strict_target.py`

`format_strict_target_progress()` renders one of:
- `Strict target: Y.Y/100 · currently X.X/100 (Z.Z below target)` — yellow
- `Strict target: Y.Y/100 · currently X.X/100 (Z.Z above target)` — green
- `Strict target: Y.Y/100 · on target` — green

This function is shared between:
- `print_score_update()` in `score_update.py` (used when `config` is passed)
- `show_strict_target_progress()` in `scan_reporting_summary.py` (available for scan use, but not actually called from `cmd_scan`)

### 7. LLM / agent block (scan only, agent environments)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_llm.py` (line 258-338)

`_print_llm_summary()` — only fires when `CLAUDE_CODE`, `DESLOPPIFY_AGENT`, or similar env vars are set. Prints all four scores in plain text format plus a dimension table and workflow guide. Includes the "north star" mention in the score legend. Does not print a target value.

---

## Summary of Whether Target Score Is Already Displayed

| Surface | Shows target score? |
|---|---|
| `scan` — `show_score_delta()` | No |
| `scan` — `show_post_scan_analysis()` | No |
| `status` — `score_summary_lines()` | No |
| `next` — `render_followup_nudges()` | **Yes** — "North star: strict X.X/100 → target Y.Y" |
| `fix` — `print_score_update()` | No (config not passed) |
| `resolve` — `print_score_update()` | No (config not passed) |
| `review import` — `print_score_update()` | **Yes** (config is passed, triggers `format_strict_target_progress`) |
| `review merge` — `print_score_update()` | No (config not passed) |
| LLM block (agent only) | No |

## Key Observations

1. The target score (`target_strict_score` from config, defaulting to 95.0) is only surfaced in two places: the `next` command's "North star" footer, and the `review import` command via `print_score_update(config=config)`.

2. The `scan` command computes `target_value` and even passes it to `show_post_scan_analysis`, but that function never prints it to the terminal. The `show_strict_target_progress()` function exists in `scan_reporting_summary.py` and is imported by `scan.py`, but is never called from `cmd_scan`.

3. The centralized `print_score_update()` in `score_update.py` is the single mechanism for post-mutation score display, and it has the machinery to show target progress — but `fix` and `resolve` don't pass `config`, so they never trigger it.

4. "Next command" suggestions appear in the `resolve` command via `_print_next_command()`, in `scan` via `show_post_scan_analysis()` (which always prints "`Run desloppify next`"), and implicitly in the `next` command itself through queue metadata. They do not appear alongside any score display in `fix`.

tool_result Bash
```
title:	Active Planning Mode: make plan a living document (reorder + context + next integration)
state:	OPEN
author:	peteromallet
labels:	
comments:	2
assignees:	
projects:	
milestone:	
number:	153
--
## Summary
`desloppify plan` is currently a generated snapshot, but users need it to behave like a **living working document** they can drive day-to-day. We should support a workflow where the only loop is:

1. make/update plan
2. do next plan item
3. update plan

with explicit support for reordering priority and adding meta context/rationale as reality changes.

## Problem
Today, planning is powerful but still mostly read-only:
- `plan` renders ranked queue output from findings + subjective threshold.
- ordering is algorithmic (tier/confidence/weight), not user-steerable in a durable way.
- there is no first-class way to attach planning context (intent, blockers, hypotheses, decomposition notes) to plan items.
- `next` has strong filters/groups, but not a persistent "manual planning order" mode.

This makes it harder to run an intentional architecture/refactor campaign where priorities need to shift as deeper causes are discovered.

## Desired workflow (single-path execution)
A user should be able to stay inside these commands only:
- `desloppify plan` (create/sync/update living plan)
- `desloppify next` (pull next action from that plan)
- `desloppify resolve ...` (record outcomes)
- back to `desloppify plan` (reorder/reframe/annotate)

No external docs should be required to keep strategic context.

## Proposal: Active Planning Mode
Introduce a first-class active planning layer with durable editing controls.

### 1) Persistent plan state
Add `.desloppify/plan.json` as source-of-truth for active plan items, and keep `plan.md` as a render target.

Each plan item should include:
- `id`
- `source_finding_ids` (one or more)
- `kind` (`finding`, `meta_task`, `refactor_epic`, `review_followup`)
- `priority_rank` (manual override)
- `status` (`open`, `in_progress`, `blocked`, `done`, `dropped`)
- `context` object:
  - `why_now`
  - `expected_impact`
  - `blockers`
  - `notes`
  - `owner`
  - `updated_at`
- `links` (related finding IDs, detector families, dimensions)

### 2) Reordering and plan editing commands
Add explicit plan-edit commands, e.g.:
- `desloppify plan edit --move <item-id> --before <item-id>`
- `desloppify plan edit --move <item-id> --after <item-id>`
- `desloppify plan edit --pin-top <item-id>`
- `desloppify plan edit --set-status <item-id> in_progress`
- `desloppify plan edit --note <item-id> "..."`
- `desloppify plan add --type meta_task --title "Investigate root cause of ..."`

### 3) Plan/queue sync semantics
Add `desloppify plan sync` that:
- refreshes findings from latest state,
- preserves manual ordering/context where source IDs still exist,
- marks missing findings as `resolved/obsolete` rather than silently dropping,
- surfaces conflicts (e.g. one plan item mapped to multiple replaced findings).

### 4) `next` integration with active plan
Support:
- `desloppify next --from-plan` (default when active plan exists)
- `desloppify next --from-queue` (current behavior fallback)
- transparent hint when queue ranking and manual plan ranking diverge.

### 5) Meta-planning / root-cause support
Enable plan items that are not single findings:
- architecture epics
- detector-family root causes
- consolidation/refactor tracks

Add optional command:
- `desloppify plan suggest-meta`
that proposes higher-level tasks by clustering repeated/related findings across modules.

### 6) Context visibility
Ensure `plan` output always shows:
- item rationale
- blockers
- last update timestamp
- related dimension drag / detector impact

This makes the plan self-documenting and usable as the primary execution surface.

## UX requirements
- Fast single-command operations (no hidden/complex flow).
- Safe defaults and obvious output after every edit.
- Works in non-interactive CLI mode; optional TUI can come later.

## Acceptance criteria
- A user can reorder any plan item and the order persists across scans.
- A user can add/update context notes on any plan item.
- `next --from-plan` follows manual plan order, not just algorithmic queue rank.
- `plan sync` preserves context and reports mapping/conflict events.
- Plan supports both finding-level and meta-level items.
- The normal fix loop can be run end-to-end using only `plan`, `next`, and `resolve`.

## Why this matters
This closes the gap between "scanner output" and "actual engineering execution". It also makes desloppify better for long-running refactors, where strategy needs to evolve as we discover deeper causes.
```

tool_result Bash
```
title:	Gate subjective reruns behind a drained plan + auto-clear stale subjective plan items
state:	OPEN
author:	peteromallet
labels:	
comments:	0
assignees:	
projects:	
milestone:	
number:	157
--
## Summary
Subjective review reruns should be **end-of-loop work**, not something we do while active plan items are still open.

Requested workflow policy:
- Before rerunning subjective review, the active plan should be fully cleared.
- If stale subjective review entries are still hanging around, clear them out first so they don’t block or pollute the next cycle.

## Why this matters
Right now it’s possible to rerun subjective review while the plan still has unresolved work. That causes churn:
- open queue keeps changing while we’re still executing old commitments,
- stale subjective markers can keep reappearing and steal attention,
- teams get pulled into review cycles too early instead of finishing the current plan.

This hurts momentum and makes the plan feel less trustworthy as the source of truth.

## Desired behavior
### 1) Plan-drained precondition for subjective rerun
When running:
- `desloppify review --prepare`
- `desloppify review --run-batches ...`
- `desloppify review --external-start ...`

if there are still open actionable plan items, block rerun by default and print:
- open item count,
- top remaining items,
- exact commands to finish or resolve them.

### 2) Clear stale subjective entries before rerun
Before starting a new subjective cycle:
- detect stale subjective review plan items/findings from previous cycle,
- mark/archive/resolve them in a deterministic way,
- report exactly what was cleared and why.

Goal: new subjective run starts from a clean queue state, not a mixed stale+fresh state.

### 3) Keep an explicit escape hatch
Provide override for advanced users/emergencies, e.g.:
- `--force-review-rerun`

Default should remain strict: no rerun until plan is clear.

## Scope proposal
- Add a shared preflight guard used by all subjective review entry paths.
- Add stale-subjective cleanup routine invoked in that preflight.
- Integrate with plan/queue rendering so users can see when they are blocked and what to do next.

## Acceptance criteria
1. Subjective rerun commands are blocked when plan has open actionable items.
2. CLI output explains blocker counts and next commands clearly.
3. Stale subjective plan entries are auto-cleared (or clearly archived) before rerun.
4. A forced override exists and is explicit in output when used.
5. Tests cover:
   - blocked rerun with open plan,
   - allowed rerun with empty plan,
   - stale cleanup behavior,
   - override path.

## Related
- #156 (stale subjective marker re-open behavior)
- #153 (active planning mode / durable plan workflow)
```

    46→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    47→    original_id: Required[str]
    48→    original_detector: str
    49→    original_file: str
    50→    original_summary: str
    51→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    52→    superseded_at: str
    53→    remapped_to: str | None
    54→    candidates: list[str]
    55→    note: str | None
    56→
    57→
    58→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    59→    version: Required[int]
    60→    created: Required[str]
    61→    updated: Required[str]
    62→    queue_order: list[str]
    63→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    64→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    65→    active_cluster: str | None
    66→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    67→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    68→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    69→
    70→
    71→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    72→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    73→    now = utc_now()
    74→    return {
    75→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    76→        "created": now,
    77→        "updated": now,
    78→        "queue_order": [],
    79→        "deferred": [],
    80→        "skipped": {},
    81→        "active_cluster": None,
    82→        "overrides": {},
    83→        "clusters": {},
    84→        "superseded": {},
    85→    }
    86→
    87→
    88→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    89→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    90→
    91→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    92→    """
    93→    defaults = empty_plan()
    94→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    95→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    96→
    97→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    98→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   100→        plan["deferred"] = []
   101→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   102→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   103→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   104→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   105→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   106→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   107→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   108→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   109→
   110→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   111→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   112→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   113→    if deferred:
   114→        now = utc_now()
   115→        for fid in list(deferred):
   116→            if fid not in skipped:
   117→                skipped[fid] = {
   118→                    "finding_id": fid,
   119→                    "kind": "temporary",
   120→                    "reason": None,
   121→                    "note": None,
   122→                    "attestation": None,
   123→                    "created_at": now,
   124→                    "review_after": None,
   125→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   126→                }
   127→        deferred.clear()
   128→
   129→    # Normalize cluster fields
   130→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   131→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   132→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   133→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   134→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   135→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   136→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   137→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   138→
   139→
   140→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   141→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   142→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   143→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   144→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   145→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   146→
   147→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   148→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   149→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   150→    if overlap:
   151→        raise ValueError(
   152→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   153→        )
   154→
   155→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   156→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   157→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   158→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   159→            raise ValueError(
   160→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   161→            )
   162→
   163→
   164→__all__ = [
   165→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   166→    "Cluster",
   167→    "ItemOverride",
   168→    "PlanModel",
   169→    "SkipEntry",
   170→    "SupersededEntry",
   171→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   172→    "empty_plan",
   173→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   174→    "validate_plan",
   175→]
   176→

   111→        dispatch = {

```
title:	show <mechanical-dimension> is misclassified as subjective when no findings match
state:	OPEN
author:	peteromallet
labels:	
comments:	0
assignees:	
projects:	
milestone:	
number:	167
--
## Summary
`desloppify show <pattern>` currently reports some **mechanical** dimensions as "subjective" when no direct findings match the pattern.

I can reproduce this with `security` and `duplication` in a Python project.

## Environment
- desloppify: `0.7.8`
- Project scanned: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP`

## Repro
```bash
desloppify scan --path . --skip-slow
# output includes: "security: 1530 findings"

desloppify show security
```

Actual output:
- Prints score line for `Security`
- Then prints:
  - `'security' is a subjective dimension — its score comes from design reviews, not code findings.`

Also reproducible with:
```bash
desloppify show duplication
```
which prints duplication score and then the same subjective-dimension message.

## Expected
For mechanical dimensions (`Security`, `Duplication`, etc.):
- Either show matching findings (if any),
- or show a mechanical-dimension summary/help,
- but **do not** label them as subjective dimensions.

## Likely root cause
In `desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py`:
- `display_name` is populated from `state["dimension_scores"]` keys (e.g. `Security`).
- The branch currently treats `if display_name` as subjective, regardless of whether the dimension is mechanical.
- This makes mechanical dimensions fall into the subjective handler whenever there are no direct matches.

The condition should only route to subjective messaging if the dimension key is actually subjective (e.g. via `subjective_assessment`/`subjective_review` detector mapping), not merely because a display name exists.

## Impact
- Misleading CLI guidance while triaging findings.
- Confusing when scan reports large security finding counts but `show security` claims subjective-only behavior.
```

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    for fid in finding_ids:
   147→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   148→        skipped[fid] = {
   149→            "finding_id": fid,
   150→            "kind": kind,
   151→            "reason": reason,
   152→            "note": note,
   153→            "attestation": attestation,
   154→            "created_at": now,
   155→            "review_after": review_after,
   156→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   157→        }
   158→        count += 1
   159→    return count
   160→
   161→
   162→def unskip_items(
   163→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   164→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   165→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   166→
   167→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   168→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   169→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   170→    """
   171→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   172→    count = 0
   173→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   174→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   177→        if entry is not None:
   178→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   179→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   180→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   181→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   182→            count += 1
   183→    return count, need_reopen
   184→
   185→
   186→def resurface_stale_skips(
   187→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   188→) -> list[str]:
   189→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   190→
   191→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   192→    """
   193→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   194→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   195→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   196→    for fid in list(skipped):
   197→        entry = skipped[fid]
   198→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   199→            continue
   200→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   201→        if review_after is None:
   202→            continue
   203→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   204→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   205→            skipped.pop(fid)
   206→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   207→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   208→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   209→    return resurfaced
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# Describe / annotate
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→def describe_finding(
   217→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   220→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   221→    now = utc_now()
   222→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   223→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   224→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   225→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   226→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   227→
   228→
   229→def annotate_finding(
   230→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   231→) -> None:
   232→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   233→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   236→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   237→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   238→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   239→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Clusters
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def create_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel,
   248→    name: str,
   249→    description: str | None = None,
   250→    action: str | None = None,
   251→) -> Cluster:
   252→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   253→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   254→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   255→        raise ValueError(
   256→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   257→        )
   258→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   259→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   260→    now = utc_now()
   261→    cluster: Cluster = {
   262→        "name": name,
   263→        "description": description,
   264→        "finding_ids": [],
   265→        "created_at": now,
   266→        "updated_at": now,
   267→        "auto": False,
   268→        "cluster_key": "",
   269→        "action": action,
   270→        "user_modified": False,
   271→    }
   272→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   273→    return cluster
   274→
   275→
   276→def add_to_cluster(
   277→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   278→) -> int:
   279→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   280→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   281→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   282→    if cluster is None:
   283→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   284→
   285→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   286→    count = 0
   287→    now = utc_now()
   288→    for fid in finding_ids:
   289→        if fid not in member_ids:
   290→            member_ids.append(fid)
   291→            count += 1
   292→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   293→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   294→        if fid not in overrides:
   295→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   296→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   297→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   298→
   299→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   300→    return count
   301→
   302→
   303→def remove_from_cluster(
   304→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   305→) -> int:
   306→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   307→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   308→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   309→    if cluster is None:
   310→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   311→
   312→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   313→    now = utc_now()
   314→    count = 0
   315→    for fid in finding_ids:
   316→        if fid in member_ids:
   317→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   318→            count += 1
   319→        # Clear cluster from override
   320→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   321→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   322→            override["cluster"] = None
   323→            override["updated_at"] = now
   324→
   325→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   326→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   327→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   328→
   329→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   330→    return count
   331→
   332→
   333→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   334→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   335→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   336→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   337→    if cluster is None:
   338→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   339→
   340→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   341→    now = utc_now()
   342→    for fid in orphaned:
   343→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   344→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   345→            override["cluster"] = None
   346→            override["updated_at"] = now
   347→
   348→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   349→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   350→
   351→    return orphaned
   352→
   353→
   354→def move_cluster(
   355→    plan: PlanModel,
   356→    cluster_name: str,
   357→    position: str,
   358→    target: str | None = None,
   359→    offset: int | None = None,
   360→) -> int:
   361→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   362→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   363→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   364→    if cluster is None:
   365→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   366→
   367→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   368→    if not member_ids:
   369→        return 0
   370→
   371→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   372→
   373→
   374→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   375→# Focus
   376→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   377→
   378→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   379→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   380→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   381→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   382→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   383→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   384→
   385→
   386→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   387→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   388→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   389→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   390→
   391→
   392→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   393→# Reset
   394→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   395→
   396→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   397→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   398→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   399→    plan.clear()
   400→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   401→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   402→        plan[k] = v
   403→    plan["created"] = created
   404→
   405→
   406→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   407→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   408→
   409→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   410→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   411→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   412→    """
   413→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   414→    found = 0
   415→
   416→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   417→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   418→    for fid in finding_ids:
   419→        was_present = False
   420→        if fid in order:
   421→            order.remove(fid)
   422→            was_present = True
   423→        if fid in skipped:
   424→            skipped.pop(fid)
   425→            was_present = True
   426→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   427→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   428→            if fid in ids:
   429→                ids.remove(fid)
   430→                was_present = True
   431→        if was_present:
   432→            found += 1
   433→
   434→    return found
   435→
   436→
   437→__all__ = [
   438→    "add_to_cluster",
   439→    "annotate_finding",
   440→    "clear_focus",
   441→    "create_cluster",
   442→    "delete_cluster",
   443→    "describe_finding",
   444→    "move_cluster",
   445→    "move_items",
   446→    "purge_ids",
   447→    "remove_from_cluster",
   448→    "reset_plan",
   449→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   450→    "set_focus",
   451→    "skip_items",
   452→    "unskip_items",
   453→]
   454→
