1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    92→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    93→        if finding_id in ids:
    94→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    95→
    96→    return True
    97→
    98→
    99→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   100→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   101→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   102→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   103→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   104→
   105→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   106→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   107→        try:
   108→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   109→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   110→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   111→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   112→                to_prune.append(fid)
   113→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   114→            to_prune.append(fid)
   115→
   116→    for fid in to_prune:
   117→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   118→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   119→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   120→
   121→    return to_prune
   122→
   123→
   124→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> ReconcileResult:
   128→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   129→
   130→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   131→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   132→    """
   133→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   134→    result = ReconcileResult()
   135→    now = utc_now()
   136→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   137→
   138→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   139→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   140→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   142→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   143→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   144→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   145→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   146→
   147→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   148→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   149→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   150→    referenced_ids = {
   151→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   152→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   153→    }
   154→
   155→    # Check each referenced ID
   156→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   157→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   158→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   159→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   160→                result.changes += 1
   161→
   162→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   163→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   164→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   165→
   166→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   167→    if resurfaced:
   168→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   169→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   170→
   171→    # Prune old superseded entries
   172→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   173→    result.pruned = pruned
   174→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   175→
   176→    return result
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "ReconcileResult",
   181→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   182→]
   183→

tool_result Bash
```
title:	Ignore/exclude pattern changes can corrupt persisted score accounting; require rebaseline scan
state:	OPEN
author:	peteromallet
labels:	
comments:	0
assignees:	
projects:	
milestone:	
number:	168
--
## Summary
Adding a broad ignore/exclusion pattern can leave persisted state in an inconsistent scoring posture (large score collapse) unless the user manually rebuilds state from a fresh scan file.

I hit this in `Headless-Wan2GP` with:

```bash
desloppify ignore "Wan2GP/**" --attest "I have actually confirmed Wan2GP is treated as vendor scope in this repository and I am not gaming the score by suppressing first-party debt."
```

Immediate output included:

- `Removed 4999 matching findings from state.`
- strict/objective dropped abruptly (about `78.5/86.7` -> `70.3/66.2`)

A normal `desloppify scan --path . --skip-slow` did **not** recover. A fresh-state scan (`--state /tmp/...`) did recover objective health, which indicates a persisted-state accounting issue rather than detector output quality.

## Why this looks like a product bug (not one-off local flake)
There are two state mutation behaviors that make this reproducible:

1. `ignore` mutates persisted findings in-place before a fresh scan reconciliation.
   - `cmd_ignore_pattern` calls `remove_ignored_findings(...)` directly and recomputes scores immediately.
   - Code: `desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py` (around lines 156-186).

2. `remove_ignored_findings` reopens resolved findings (`fixed/auto_resolved/false_positive` -> `open`) for matched patterns.
   - Code: `desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py` (around lines 107-132).
   - `_recompute_stats(...)` then counts by `status` (suppressed still counted).
   - Code: `desloppify/engine/_state/scoring.py` (around lines 38-50).

Minimal local repro (on current `main`, 0.7.8) demonstrates reopen + strict collapse:

```python
from desloppify.state import empty_state, merge_scan, MergeScanOptions, remove_ignored_findings

st = empty_state()
finding = {
  'id':'unused::Wan2GP/a.py::x','detector':'unused','file':'Wan2GP/a.py',
  'tier':2,'confidence':'high','summary':'x','detail':{},'status':'open',
  'first_seen':'2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00','last_seen':'2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00',
  'resolved_at':None,'reopen_count':0,
}
merge_scan(st,[finding],MergeScanOptions(lang='python',potentials={'unused':1},force_resolve=True))
merge_scan(st,[],MergeScanOptions(lang='python',potentials={'unused':1},force_resolve=True))
print(st['findings']['unused::Wan2GP/a.py::x']['status'])  # auto_resolved
print(st['strict_score'])                                   # 40.0
remove_ignored_findings(st,'Wan2GP/**')
print(st['findings']['unused::Wan2GP/a.py::x']['status'])  # open
print(st['strict_score'])                                   # 0.0
```

## Expected vs actual
### Expected
- Changing ignore/exclude configuration should produce deterministic, explainable score transitions.
- Broad path suppression should not leave long-lived stale/open debt accounting that requires manual state surgery to recover.
- CLI messaging should be accurate about what happened.

### Actual
- Large pattern updates can trigger severe score collapse from persisted-state mutation semantics.
- Recovery may require full fresh-state rebuild + manual subjective-state carryover.
- Message says `Removed ... matching findings from state`, but findings are marked suppressed (not actually removed), and some resolved findings are reopened as open.

## Proposed fix (guardrail)
When `ignore` or `exclude` config changes:

1. Mark state as `requires_rebaseline_scan` with reason + config diff hash.
2. Block `status/strict score` as authoritative until a rescan completes OR auto-run a scan if path/lang is unambiguous.
3. Rebuild scored view from fresh detector output in that post-change scan (transactional), instead of in-place status rewrites against historical findings.
4. Update UX text:
   - Replace `Removed N matching findings from state` with precise language (`Suppressed N findings` + `Reopened M resolved findings for score integrity` if still keeping that behavior).
5. Add regression tests:
   - broad ignore added after many resolved findings
   - exclude list changed between scans
   - score stability guarantees after mandatory rebaseline

## Nice-to-have
- Add explicit command like `desloppify rebaseline --reason "ignore/exclude changed"` to make this flow obvious and auditable.
```

> TOOL

tool_result Bash
```
title:	Review rubric: enforce root-cause findings for low score bands
state:	OPEN
author:	peteromallet
labels:	
comments:	0
assignees:	
projects:	
milestone:	
number:	147
--
## Problem

In subjective review workflows, low scores can still produce mostly symptom-level findings (or too few findings), which makes remediation loops unfocused. This is not specific to one repo/language; it appears to be a rubric/validation gap.

The core issue is a mismatch between:
- **score severity** (e.g. <70), and
- **finding depth/coverage** (e.g. only local nits, or missing major/root-cause issue).

That mismatch weakens triage quality and can send users into patching symptoms instead of fixing architecture.

## Proposal (generic)

### 1) Add score-band finding contracts

Make finding requirements configurable but with sensible defaults:
- If a dimension score is `< 85`: require at least one actionable finding tied to that dimension.
- If a dimension score is `< 70`: require at least one **significant/root-cause** finding for that dimension.
- If overall strict score is `< 70`: require at least one **cross-module/architectural** finding in the review set.

### 2) Add root-cause classification to findings

Extend finding schema with normalized fields:
- `severity_tier` (`t1`..`t4`)
- `scope` (`local`, `module`, `cross_module`, `system`)
- `cause_level` (`symptom`, `root_cause`)
- `dimension` (which subjective dimension it belongs to)
- `impact` (brief statement of why score is dragged down)

This allows deterministic validation without hard-coding detector names.

### 3) Validate score/finding consistency on import

During `review --import`, fail (or strict-warn) when score/finding combinations are inconsistent, e.g.:
- score `< 70` but no `cause_level=root_cause`
- score `< 85` but zero findings for that dimension
- only low-tier findings with very low score

This should return explicit actionable error messages to the reviewer.

### 4) Prompt/rubric updates for reviewers

Reviewer instructions should explicitly state:
- major refactors are allowed when warranted,
- at least one root-cause issue is expected for low dimensions,
- findings should explain what blocks higher scores (not just list risks).

### 5) Keep it configurable

Expose thresholds in config so teams can tune sensitivity:
- `root_cause_threshold` (default 70)
- `minimum_findings_threshold` (default 85)
- validation mode: `warn | fail`

## Why this helps

- Produces higher-signal remediation plans.
- Reduces “symptom-chasing” loops.
- Makes subjective review outputs more reliable across repos/languages.
- Keeps behavior generic and policy-driven rather than project-specific.

## Acceptance criteria

- Import rejects (or warns, based on config) low-score reviews that do not include required root-cause coverage.
- At least one architectural/cross-module issue is required when strict score is below configured threshold.
- Error messages clearly explain which score-band contract failed.
- Works uniformly across TypeScript, Python, and C# review flows.
```

    33→
    34→def _show_concerns(state: dict, lang_name: str | None) -> None:
    35→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
    36→    from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
    37→
    38→    concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
    39→    if not concerns:
    40→        print(colorize("  No design concerns detected.", "green"))
    41→        return
    42→
    43→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(concerns)} design concern(s):\n", "bold"))
    44→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
    45→
    46→    for i, c in enumerate(concerns, 1):
    47→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{c.type}] {c.file}", "cyan"))
    48→        print(f"     {c.summary}")
    49→        for ev in c.evidence:
    50→            print(colorize(f"       - {ev}", "dim"))
    51→        print(colorize(f"     ? {c.question}", "yellow"))
    52→
    53→        # Check if previously dismissed (but resurface due to changed findings).
    54→        prev = dismissals.get(c.fingerprint)
    55→        if isinstance(prev, dict):
    56→            reasoning = prev.get("reasoning", "")
    57→            if reasoning:
    58→                print(colorize(f"     (previously dismissed: {reasoning})", "dim"))
    59→        print()
    60→
    61→
    62→def _print_dimension_score(dim_data: dict, display_name: str) -> None:
    63→    """Print the health/strict score line for a dimension if available."""
    64→    score_val = dim_data.get("score") if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else None
    65→    strict_val = (
    66→        dim_data.get("strict", score_val) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else None
    67→    )
    68→    if score_val is not None:
    69→        print(
    70→            colorize(
    71→                f"  {display_name}: {score_val:.1f}% health (strict: {strict_val:.1f}%)",
    72→                "bold",
    73→            )
    74→        )
    75→
    76→
    77→def _render_subjective_dimension(
    78→    state: dict,
    79→    config: dict,
    80→    entity,
    81→    pattern_raw: str,
    82→) -> None:
    83→    """Show score + subjective explanation for a subjective dimension."""
    84→    lowered = pattern_raw.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_") if pattern_raw else ""
    85→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, entity.display_name)
    86→    _print_dimension_score(dim_data, display_name)
    87→    print(
    88→        colorize(
    89→            f"  '{pattern_raw.strip()}' is a subjective dimension "
    90→            "— its score comes from design reviews, not code findings.",
    91→            "yellow",
    92→        )
    93→    )
    94→    # Count open review findings tagged with this dimension
    95→    dim_reviews = [
    96→        f
    97→        for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
    98→        if f.get("detector") == "review"
    99→        and f.get("status") == "open"
   100→        and lowered
   101→        in str(f.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   102→    ]
   103→    if dim_reviews:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                f"  {len(dim_reviews)} open review finding(s). "
   107→                "Run `show review --status open`.",
   108→                "dim",
   109→            )
   110→        )
   111→    show_subjective_followup(
   112→        state,
   113→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   114→    )
   115→
   116→
   117→def _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state: dict, entity) -> None:
   118→    """Show score + 'no open findings' for a mechanical dimension with zero findings."""
   119→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, entity.display_name)
   120→    _print_dimension_score(dim_data, display_name)
   121→    det_list = ", ".join(entity.detectors) if entity.detectors else "none"
   122→    print(
   123→        colorize(
   124→            f"  No open findings for {entity.display_name}. Detectors: {det_list}",
   125→            "green",
   126→        )
   127→    )
   128→
   129→
   130→def _load_dimension_findings(
   131→    state: dict,
   132→    entity,
   133→    status_filter: str,
   134→) -> list[dict]:
   135→    """Load findings for all detectors in a mechanical dimension."""
   136→    all_matches: list[dict] = []
   137→    for detector in entity.detectors:
   138→        matches = load_matches(
   139→            state, scope=detector, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=False
   140→        )
   141→        all_matches.extend(matches)
   142→    # Deduplicate by finding ID
   143→    seen: set[str] = set()
   144→    unique: list[dict] = []
   145→    for item in all_matches:
   146→        fid = item.get("id", "")
   147→        if fid not in seen:
   148→            seen.add(fid)
   149→            unique.append(item)
   150→    return unique
   151→
   152→
   153→def _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config):
   154→    """Handle the no-findings case: subjective views show dashboard, others show hint."""
   155→    print(
   156→        colorize(f"No {status_filter} findings matching: {pattern}", "yellow")
   157→    )
   158→    write_query(
   159→        {
   160→            "command": "show",
   161→            "query": pattern,
   162→            "status_filter": status_filter,
   163→            "total": 0,
   164→            "findings": [],
   165→            "narrative": narrative,
   166→        }
   167→    )
   168→    if entity.kind == "special_view":
   169→        show_subjective_followup(
   170→            state,
   171→            target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   172→        )
   173→    else:
   174→        hint = _detector_names_hint()
   175→        print(
   176→            colorize(
   177→                f"  Try: show <detector>, show <file>, or show subjective. "
   178→                f"Detectors: {hint}",
   179→                "dim",
   180→            )
   181→        )
   182→
   183→
   184→def _render_subjective_views_guide(entity) -> None:
   185→    """Print 'Related views' hint after subjective/subjective_review output."""
   186→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() in (
   187→        "subjective",
   188→        "subjective_review",
   189→    ):
   190→        print(colorize("  Related views:", "dim"))
   191→        print(colorize("    `show review --status open`            Per-file design review findings", "dim"))
   192→        print(colorize("    `show subjective_review --status open`  Files needing re-review", "dim"))
   193→
   194→
   195→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   196→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   197→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   198→    state = runtime.state
   199→    config = runtime.config
   200→
   201→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   202→        return
   203→
   204→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
   205→    if stale_warning:
   206→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
   207→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   208→    if skill_warning:
   209→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   210→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   211→    if config_warning:
   212→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   213→
   214→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
   215→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
   216→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   217→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   218→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   219→        return
   220→
   221→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   222→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   223→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   224→        state,
   225→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   226→    )
   227→
   228→    # --- Entity resolution: classify what the user asked for ---
   229→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
   230→
   231→    # Dispatch: concerns special view
   232→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
   233→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
   234→        return
   235→
   236→    # Dispatch: subjective dimension
   237→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
   238→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    # Dispatch: mechanical dimension
   242→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
   243→        matches = _load_dimension_findings(state, entity, status_filter)
   244→        if not matches:
   245→            _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
   246→            return
   247→        pattern = entity.display_name
   248→    else:
   249→        # All other kinds: existing load_matches path
   250→        matches = load_matches(
   251→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    if not matches:
   255→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
   256→        return
   257→
   258→    (
   259→        surfaced_matches,
   260→        hidden_by_detector,
   261→        noise_budget,
   262→        global_noise_budget,
   263→        budget_warning,
   264→    ) = resolve_noise(
   265→        config,
   266→        matches,
   267→    )
   268→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   269→
   270→    payload = build_show_payload(
   271→        surfaced_matches,
   272→        pattern,
   273→        status_filter,
   274→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   275→            total_matches=len(matches),
   276→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   277→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   278→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   279→        ),
   280→    )
   281→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   282→
   283→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   284→    if output_file:
   285→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   286→            return
   287→        raise SystemExit(1)
   288→
   289→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   290→    render_findings(
   291→        surfaced_matches,
   292→        pattern=pattern,
   293→        status_filter=status_filter,

   162→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

title:	Feature: per-language scorecards for mixed-language repositories
state:	OPEN
author:	wang93wei
labels:	
comments:	1
assignees:	
projects:	
milestone:	
number:	140
--
## Summary
Feature request: for multi-language repositories, `desloppify` should generate **per-language score panels/scorecard images** in addition to the aggregate codebase score.

## Project Link
- Example mixed-language project: https://github.com/wang93wei/SwiftMTP
- Languages in use: Swift + Go

## Context
This project contains multiple languages (Swift + Go). We run language-specific scans such as:

```bash
desloppify --lang go scan --path Native
```

But in day-to-day workflow, teams also run whole-repo scans (`--path .`). In mixed-language repos, a single aggregate score can hide debt in one language while another language remains healthy.

## Why this matters (aligned with current tool guidance)
`desloppify` scan output includes the instruction block for agents:
- "ALWAYS present ALL scores to the user after a scan"
- show overall/objective/strict/verified
- show all mechanical + subjective dimensions

For multi-language repositories, this same principle should apply **per language**, not only globally.

## Current behavior
- Aggregate scorecard is generated (for current scan state).
- Users can force one language via `--lang`, but there is no first-class multi-language breakdown artifact from one run.

## Expected behavior
When scanning a mixed-language repo, provide language-separated reporting artifacts, for example:

1. Per-language score sections in CLI output (Swift/Go/TypeScript/etc), each with:
   - overall/objective/strict/verified
   - mechanical + subjective dimensions
2. Per-language scorecard image files, e.g.:
   - `scorecard-go.png`
   - `scorecard-swift.png`
3. Optional aggregate image remains available (`scorecard.png`).
4. `status` should expose the same per-language breakdown without requiring separate manual runs per language.

## Suggested CLI shape (example)
- `desloppify scan --path . --by-language`
- `desloppify status --by-language`
- `desloppify scan --path . --badge-path scorecard-{lang}.png --by-language`

## Acceptance criteria
- In a repo with >=2 detected languages, `--by-language` outputs distinct score blocks and artifacts per language.
- Each language block follows current score-reporting contract (overall/objective/strict/verified + all dimensions).
- Aggregate + per-language outputs are both available and clearly labeled.
- State interactions are deterministic (no ambiguity between global state and language-scoped state).

## Notes
Related reliability friction we also observed while running language-focused review batches is tracked separately in issue #139.
```

1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
     9→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    10→    "path_scoped_findings",
    11→    "is_ignored",
    12→    "matched_ignore_pattern",
    13→    "remove_ignored_findings",
    14→    "add_ignore",
    15→    "make_finding",
    16→]
    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    19→    Finding,
    20→    StateModel,
    21→    ensure_state_defaults,
    22→    utc_now,
    23→    validate_state_invariants,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    26→
    27→
    28→def path_scoped_findings(
    29→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    30→    scan_path: str | None,
    31→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    32→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    33→    return {
    34→        finding_id: finding
    35→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    36→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    43→        return True
    44→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    45→    return (
    46→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    47→        or file_path == scan_path
    48→        or file_path == "."
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def open_scope_breakdown(
    53→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    54→    scan_path: str | None,
    55→    *,
    56→    detector: str | None = None,
    57→) -> dict[str, int]:
    58→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    59→    in_scope = 0
    60→    out_of_scope = 0
    61→
    62→    for finding in findings.values():
    63→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    64→            continue
    65→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    66→            continue
    67→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    68→            continue
    69→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    70→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    71→            in_scope += 1
    72→        else:
    73→            out_of_scope += 1
    74→
    75→    return {
    76→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    77→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    78→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    79→    }
    80→
    81→
    82→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    83→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    84→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    85→
    86→
    87→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    88→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    89→) -> str | None:
    90→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    91→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    92→        if "*" in pattern:
    93→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    94→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    95→                return pattern
    96→            continue
    97→
    98→        if "::" in pattern:
    99→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   100→                return pattern
   101→            continue
   102→
   103→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   104→            return pattern
   105→
   106→    return None
   107→
   108→
   109→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   110→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   111→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   112→    matched_ids = [
   113→        finding_id
   114→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   115→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   116→    ]
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   119→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   120→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   121→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   122→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   123→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   124→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   125→
   126→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   127→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   128→    return len(matched_ids)
   129→
   130→
   131→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   132→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   133→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   134→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   135→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   136→    if pattern not in ignores:
   137→        ignores.append(pattern)
   138→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   139→
   140→
   141→def make_finding(
   142→    detector: str,
   143→    file: str,
   144→    name: str,
   145→    *,
   146→    tier: int,
   147→    confidence: str,
   148→    summary: str,
   149→    detail: dict | None = None,
   150→) -> Finding:
   151→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   152→    rfile = rel(file)
   153→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   154→    now = utc_now()
   155→    return {
   156→        "id": finding_id,
   157→        "detector": detector,
   158→        "file": rfile,
   159→        "tier": tier,
   160→        "confidence": confidence,
   161→        "summary": summary,
   162→        "detail": detail or {},
   163→        "status": "open",
   164→        "note": None,
   165→        "first_seen": now,
   166→        "last_seen": now,
   167→        "resolved_at": None,
   168→        "reopen_count": 0,
   169→    }
   170→
   171→
   172→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   173→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
   174→    return (
   175→        finding_id == pattern
   176→        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
   177→        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
   178→        or (
   179→            finding.get("detector") == pattern
   180→            or finding["file"] == pattern
   181→            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   182→        )
   183→    )
   184→

1→"""State scoring, statistics, and suppression accounting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from copy import deepcopy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "suppression_metrics",
     9→]
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_confidence
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import matches_target_score
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_findings
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import ScanCoverageRecord
    16→
    17→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = ("open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "wontfix", "false_positive")
    18→_SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD = 2
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):
    25→        for entry in reversed(history):
    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    29→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    35→    return None
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    45→            continue
    46→        status = finding["status"]
    47→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    48→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    50→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    51→
    52→    return counters, tier_stats
    53→
    54→
    55→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    56→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    57→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    58→    try:
    59→        score = float(raw)
    60→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    61→        score = 0.0
    62→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    63→
    64→
    65→def _subjective_target_matches(
    66→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    67→) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    69→    matches = [
    70→        dimension
    71→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    72→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    73→    ]
    74→    return sorted(matches)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    78→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    79→    return {
    80→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    81→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    82→        "matched_count": 0,
    83→        "matched_dimensions": [],
    84→        "reset_dimensions": [],
    85→    }
    86→
    87→
    88→def _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
    89→    subjective_assessments: dict,
    90→    *,
    91→    target: float,
    92→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
    93→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""
    94→    normalized_target = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(target)))
    95→    matched_dimensions = _subjective_target_matches(
    96→        subjective_assessments,
    97→        target=normalized_target,
    98→    )
    99→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)
   100→    meta["matched_count"] = len(matched_dimensions)
   101→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   102→
   103→    if len(matched_dimensions) < _SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD:
   104→        meta["status"] = "warn" if matched_dimensions else "pass"
   105→        return subjective_assessments, meta
   106→
   107→    adjusted = deepcopy(subjective_assessments)
   108→    for dimension in matched_dimensions:
   109→        payload = adjusted.get(dimension)
   110→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   111→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   112→            payload["integrity_penalty"] = "target_match_reset"
   113→        else:
   114→            adjusted[dimension] = {
   115→                "score": 0.0,
   116→                "integrity_penalty": "target_match_reset",
   117→            }
   118→
   119→    meta["status"] = "penalized"
   120→    meta["reset_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   121→    return adjusted, meta
   122→
   123→
   124→def _aggregate_scores(
   125→    dim_scores: dict, compute_health_score_fn
   126→) -> dict[str, float]:
   127→    """Derive the 4 aggregate scores from dimension-level data."""
   128→    mechanical = {
   129→        n: d
   130→        for n, d in dim_scores.items()
   131→        if "subjective_assessment" not in d.get("detectors", {})
   132→    }
   133→    return {
   134→        "overall_score": compute_health_score_fn(dim_scores),
   135→        "strict_score": compute_health_score_fn(
   136→            dim_scores, score_key="strict_score"
   137→        ),
   138→        "objective_score": compute_health_score_fn(mechanical),
   139→        "verified_strict_score": compute_health_score_fn(
   140→            mechanical, [REDACTED]
   141→        ),
   142→    }
   143→
   144→
   145→def _active_scan_coverage(state: StateModel) -> ScanCoverageRecord:
   146→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
   147→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict) or not scan_coverage:
   148→        return {}
   149→
   150→    lang_name = state.get("lang")
   151→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
   152→        payload = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
   153→        return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
   154→
   155→    if len(scan_coverage) == 1:
   156→        only = next(iter(scan_coverage.values()))
   157→        return only if isinstance(only, dict) else {}
   158→    return {}
   159→
   160→
   161→def _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   162→    state: StateModel,
   163→    *,
   164→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict],
   165→) -> None:
   166→    coverage_payload = _active_scan_coverage(state)
   167→    detectors_payload = coverage_payload.get("detectors", {})
   168→    if not isinstance(detectors_payload, dict):
   169→        state["score_confidence"] = {
   170→            "status": "full",
   171→            "confidence": 1.0,
   172→            "detectors": [],
   173→            "dimensions": [],
   174→        }
   175→        return
   176→
   177→    reduced_detectors: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   178→    for detector, raw in detectors_payload.items():
   179→        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
   180→            continue
   181→        status = str(raw.get("status", "full")).lower()
   182→        confidence = coerce_confidence(raw.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   183→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
   184→            continue
   185→        reduced_detectors[str(detector)] = dict(raw)
   186→
   187→    reduced_dimensions: list[str] = []
   188→    score_confidence_detectors: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   189→    for detector, payload in reduced_detectors.items():
   190→        score_confidence_detectors.append(
   191→            {
   192→                "detector": detector,
   193→                "status": str(payload.get("status", "reduced")),
   194→                "confidence": round(
   195→                    coerce_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0), 2
   196→                ),
   197→                "summary": str(payload.get("summary", "") or ""),
   198→                "impact": str(payload.get("impact", "") or ""),
   199→                "remediation": str(payload.get("remediation", "") or ""),
   200→                "tool": str(payload.get("tool", "") or ""),
   201→                "reason": str(payload.get("reason", "") or ""),
   202→            }
   203→        )
   204→
   205→    for dim_name, dim_data in dimension_scores.items():
   206→        if not isinstance(dim_data, dict):
   207→            continue
   208→        detectors = dim_data.get("detectors", {})
   209→        if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   210→            continue
   211→
   212→        impacts: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   213→        for detector_name, detector_meta in detectors.items():
   214→            reduced = reduced_detectors.get(str(detector_name))
   215→            if not isinstance(detector_meta, dict):
   216→                continue
   217→            if reduced is None:
   218→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_status", None)
   219→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   220→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_summary", None)
   221→                continue
   222→            confidence = coerce_confidence(reduced.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   223→            status = str(reduced.get("status", "reduced"))
   224→            summary = str(reduced.get("summary", "") or "")
   225→            detector_meta["coverage_status"] = status
   226→            detector_meta["coverage_confidence"] = round(confidence, 2)
   227→            detector_meta["coverage_summary"] = summary
   228→            impacts.append(
   229→                {
   230→                    "detector": str(detector_name),
   231→                    "status": status,
   232→                    "confidence": round(confidence, 2),
   233→                    "summary": summary,
   234→                }
   235→            )
   236→
   237→        if not impacts:
   238→            dim_data.pop("coverage_status", None)
   239→            dim_data.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   240→            dim_data.pop("coverage_impacts", None)
   241→            continue
   242→
   243→        reduced_dimensions.append(str(dim_name))
   244→        dim_data["coverage_status"] = "reduced"
   245→        dim_data["coverage_confidence"] = round(
   246→            min(coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0) for item in impacts),
   247→            2,
   248→        )
   249→        dim_data["coverage_impacts"] = impacts
   250→
   251→    if not score_confidence_detectors:
   252→        state["score_confidence"] = {
   253→            "status": "full",
   254→            "confidence": 1.0,
   255→            "detectors": [],
   256→            "dimensions": [],
   257→        }
   258→        return
   259→
   260→    state["score_confidence"] = {
   261→        "status": "reduced",
   262→        "confidence": round(
   263→            min(
   264→                coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   265→                for item in score_confidence_detectors
   266→            ),
   267→            2,
   268→        ),
   269→        "detectors": score_confidence_detectors,
   270→        "dimensions": sorted(set(reduced_dimensions)),
   271→    }
   272→
   273→
   274→def _update_objective_health(
   275→    state: StateModel,
   276→    findings: dict,
   277→    *,
   278→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   279→) -> None:
   280→    """Compute canonical score trio from dimension scoring."""
   281→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   282→    if not pots:
   283→        return
   284→
   285→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with desloppify.scoring
   286→    # (scoring -> _scoring/results/core -> _scoring/subjective/core -> review -> state -> _state/merge -> _state/scoring)
   287→    from desloppify.scoring import (
   288→        compute_health_score,
   289→        compute_score_bundle,
   290→        merge_potentials,
   291→    )
   292→
   293→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   294→    if not merged:
   295→        return
   296→
   297→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   298→    integrity_target = (
   299→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(subjective_integrity_target)))
   300→        if isinstance(subjective_integrity_target, int | float)
   301→        else None
   302→    )
   303→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   304→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   305→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   306→            subjective_assessments,
   307→            target=integrity_target,
   308→        )
   309→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   310→
   311→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   312→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   313→        state["dimension_scores"] = {}
   314→        state["overall_score"] = 100.0
   315→        state["objective_score"] = 100.0
   316→        state["strict_score"] = 100.0
   317→        state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
   318→        return
   319→
   320→    # Use the full scorecard dimensions for 0-score placeholders so every
   321→    # dimension the review workflow covers gets a placeholder when unreviewed.
   322→    # Explicit assessments outside this set still count
   323→    # (append_subjective_dimensions handles that).
   324→    allowed_subjective: set[str] | None = None
   325→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
   326→    if lang_name:
   327→        try:
   328→            from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import (
   329→                load_dimensions_for_lang,
   330→            )
   331→
   332→            dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   333→            if dims:
   334→                allowed_subjective = set(dims)
   335→        except (ImportError, AttributeError):
   336→            pass
   337→
   338→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   339→        findings,
   340→        merged,
   341→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   342→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   343→    )
   344→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   345→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   346→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   347→
   348→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   349→
   350→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   351→        name: dict(
   352→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   353→            strict_score=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   354→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   355→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   356→            issues=lenient_scores[name]["issues"],
   357→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   358→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   359→        )
   360→        for name in lenient_scores
   361→    }
   362→    for data in state["dimension_scores"].values():
   363→        data["strict"] = data["strict_score"]
   364→
   365→    # Carry forward mechanical dimensions from a prior scan that are absent
   366→    # now (e.g. duplication when --skip-slow is used).
   367→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   368→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   369→            continue
   370→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   371→            continue
   372→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   373→            continue
   374→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   375→        # Backfill for state files written before verified_strict_score existed.
   376→        carried.setdefault(
   377→            "verified_strict_score",
   378→            carried.get("strict_score", carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))),
   379→        )
   380→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   381→
   382→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   383→        state,
   384→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   385→    )
   386→
   387→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"], compute_health_score))
   388→
   389→
   390→def _recompute_stats(
   391→    state: StateModel,
   392→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   393→    *,
   394→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   395→) -> None:
   396→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from findings."""
   397→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   398→    findings = path_scoped_findings(state["findings"], scan_path)
   399→    counters, tier_stats = _count_findings(findings)
   400→    state["stats"] = {
   401→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   402→        **counters,
   403→        "by_tier": {
   404→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   405→        },
   406→    }
   407→    _update_objective_health(
   408→        state,
   409→        findings,
   410→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   411→    )
   412→
   413→
   414→def _empty_suppression_metrics() -> dict[str, int | float]:
   415→    return {
   416→        "last_ignored": 0,
   417→        "last_raw_findings": 0,
   418→        "last_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   419→        "last_ignore_patterns": 0,
   420→        "recent_scans": 0,
   421→        "recent_ignored": 0,
   422→        "recent_raw_findings": 0,
   423→        "recent_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   424→    }
   425→
   426→
   427→def suppression_metrics(state: StateModel, *, window: int = 5) -> dict[str, int | float]:
   428→    """Summarize ignore suppression from recent scan history."""
   429→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   430→    if not history:
   431→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   432→
   433→    scans_with_suppression = [
   434→        entry
   435→        for entry in history
   436→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
   437→        and (
   438→            "ignored" in entry
   439→            or "raw_findings" in entry
   440→            or "suppressed_pct" in entry
   441→            or "ignore_patterns" in entry
   442→        )
   443→    ]
   444→    if not scans_with_suppression:
   445→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   446→
   447→    recent = scans_with_suppression[-max(1, window) :]
   448→    last = recent[-1]
   449→
   450→    recent_ignored = sum(int(entry.get("ignored", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   451→    recent_raw = sum(int(entry.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   452→    recent_pct = round(recent_ignored / recent_raw * 100, 1) if recent_raw else 0.0
   453→
   454→    last_ignored = int(last.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   455→    last_raw = int(last.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   456→    if "suppressed_pct" in last:
   457→        last_pct = round(float(last.get("suppressed_pct") or 0.0), 1)
   458→    else:
   459→        last_pct = round(last_ignored / last_raw * 100, 1) if last_raw else 0.0
   460→
   461→    return {
   462→        "last_ignored": last_ignored,
   463→        "last_raw_findings": last_raw,
   464→        "last_suppressed_pct": last_pct,
   465→        "last_ignore_patterns": int(last.get("ignore_patterns", 0) or 0),
   466→        "recent_scans": len(recent),
   467→        "recent_ignored": recent_ignored,
   468→        "recent_raw_findings": recent_raw,
   469→        "recent_suppressed_pct": recent_pct,
   470→    }
   471→
```

1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    10→    QueueBuildOptions,
    11→    build_work_queue,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class ResolvedEntity:
    18→    """Classifies what the user's show pattern refers to."""
    19→
    20→    kind: str  # "dimension", "special_view", "file_or_pattern"
    21→    pattern: str  # Original user pattern
    22→    display_name: str  # Human-readable label
    23→    detectors: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Detector names (for dimension kind)
    24→    is_subjective: bool = False  # Whether this is a subjective dimension
    25→
    26→
    27→def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: dict) -> ResolvedEntity:
    28→    """Classify a user pattern as a dimension, special view, or passthrough.
    29→
    30→    Resolution priority:
    31→    1. Special views: "concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"
    32→    2. Mechanical dimension name (from DIMENSIONS)
    33→    3. Subjective dimension name (from DISPLAY_NAMES / dimension_scores)
    34→    4. Everything else: file_or_pattern passthrough
    35→    """
    36→    lowered = pattern.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    37→
    38→    # 1. Special views
    39→    if lowered in ("concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"):
    40→        return ResolvedEntity(
    41→            kind="special_view",
    42→            pattern=pattern,
    43→            display_name=pattern,
    44→        )
    45→
    46→    # 2. Mechanical dimension (via DIMENSIONS list)
    47→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    48→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered)
    49→    if not detectors:
    50→        detectors = lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    51→    if detectors:
    52→        dim_display = pattern
    53→        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    54→            if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    55→                dim_display = dim.name
    56→                break
    57→        return ResolvedEntity(
    58→            kind="dimension",
    59→            pattern=pattern,
    60→            display_name=dim_display,
    61→            detectors=tuple(detectors),
    62→            is_subjective=False,
    63→        )
    64→
    65→    # 3. Subjective dimension (via DISPLAY_NAMES or dimension_scores)
    66→    display_name = scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(lowered)
    67→    if not display_name:
    68→        for key in state.get("dimension_scores") or {}:
    69→            if key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    70→                display_name = key
    71→                break
    72→    if display_name:
    73→        dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, display_name)
    74→        is_subj = "subjective_assessment" in (
    75→            dim_data.get("detectors", {}) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else {}
    76→        )
    77→        return ResolvedEntity(
    78→            kind="dimension",
    79→            pattern=pattern,
    80→            display_name=display_name,
    81→            detectors=(),
    82→            is_subjective=is_subj,
    83→        )
    84→
    85→    # 4. Everything else
    86→    return ResolvedEntity(
    87→        kind="file_or_pattern",
    88→        pattern=pattern,
    89→        display_name=pattern,
    90→    )
    91→
    92→
    93→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    94→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
    95→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    96→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    97→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
    98→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
    99→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
   100→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   101→        if key not in lookup:
   102→            lookup[key] = detectors
   103→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
   104→    for key, display in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   105→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   106→        normalized_display = display.lower()
   107→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
   108→        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   109→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
   110→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
   111→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   112→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
   113→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
   114→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
   115→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
   116→    return lookup
   117→
   118→
   119→def _lookup_dimension_score(
   120→    state: dict, display_name: str,
   121→) -> tuple[dict, str]:
   122→    """Find dimension_scores entry with case-insensitive fallback.
   123→
   124→    Returns (dim_data_dict, resolved_display_name).
   125→    """
   126→    lowered = display_name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   127→    dim_data = (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(display_name, {})
   128→    if not dim_data:
   129→        for ds_key, ds_val in (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).items():
   130→            if ds_key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
   131→                dim_data = ds_val
   132→                display_name = ds_key
   133→                break
   134→    return dim_data, display_name
   135→
   136→
   137→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
   138→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   139→    from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
   140→    names = getattr(registry_mod, "DISPLAY_ORDER", [])
   141→    if names:
   142→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   143→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   144→
   145→
   146→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   147→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   148→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   149→    pattern = args.pattern
   150→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   151→    if chronic:
   152→        scope = pattern
   153→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   154→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   155→    if not pattern:
   156→        print(
   157→            colorize(
   158→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   159→                "yellow",
   160→            )
   161→        )
   162→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   163→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   164→
   165→
   166→def load_matches(
   167→    state: dict,
   168→    *,
   169→    scope: str | None,
   170→    status_filter: str,
   171→    chronic: bool,
   172→) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
   174→    queue = build_work_queue(
   175→        state,
   176→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   177→            count=None,
   178→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   179→            scope=scope,
   180→            status=status_filter,
   181→            include_subjective=False,
   182→            chronic=chronic,
   183→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   184→        ),
   185→    )
   186→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
   187→
   188→
   189→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict]):
   190→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches."""
   191→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   192→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   193→    )
   194→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   195→        matches,
   196→        budget=noise_budget,
   197→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   198→    )
   199→    return (
   200→        surfaced_matches,
   201→        hidden_by_detector,
   202→        noise_budget,
   203→        global_noise_budget,
   204→        budget_warning,
   205→    )
   206→
   207→
   208→__all__ = [
   209→    "ResolvedEntity",
   210→    "_detector_names_hint",
   211→    "_lookup_dimension_score",
   212→    "load_matches",
   213→    "resolve_entity",
   214→    "resolve_noise",
   215→    "resolve_show_scope",
   216→]

1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import render as next_render_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    15→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    16→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    19→    QueueBuildOptions,
    20→    build_work_queue,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    23→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness, check_tool_staleness
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    26→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    76→    if config_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→
    79→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    80→
    81→
    82→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    83→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    84→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    85→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    86→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    87→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    88→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    89→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    90→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    91→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    92→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    93→
    94→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    95→
    96→    # Load the living plan
    97→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    98→    plan = load_plan()
    99→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   100→    if (
   101→        plan.get("queue_order")
   102→        or plan.get("overrides")
   103→        or plan.get("clusters")
   104→    ):
   105→        plan_data = plan
   106→
   107→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   109→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   110→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   111→        if active_cluster:
   112→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   113→
   114→    queue = build_work_queue(
   115→        state,
   116→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   117→            tier=tier,
   118→            count=count,
   119→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   120→            scope=scope,
   121→            status=status,
   122→            include_subjective=True,
   123→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   124→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   125→            explain=explain,
   126→            plan=plan_data,
   127→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   128→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   129→        ),
   130→    )
   131→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   132→
   133→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   134→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   135→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   136→        state,
   137→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   141→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   142→    )
   143→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   144→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   145→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   147→        state,
   148→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   149→    )
   150→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   151→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   152→    ]
   153→    write_query(payload)
   154→
   155→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   156→    if output_file:
   157→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   158→            output_file,
   159→            payload,
   160→            len(items),
   161→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   162→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   163→        ):
   164→            return
   165→        raise SystemExit(1)
   166→
   167→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   171→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   172→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   176→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   177→        state.get("findings", {}),
   178→        state.get("scan_path"),
   179→    )
   180→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   183→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   184→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   186→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   187→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   188→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   189→    )
   190→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   191→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   192→        state,
   193→        dim_scores,
   194→        findings_scoped,
   195→        strict_score=strict_score,
   196→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   197→    )
   198→    print()
   199→
   200→
   201→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   202→

50→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    51→    requested_tier: int | None
    52→    selected_tier: int | None
    53→    fallback_reason: str | None
    54→    available_tiers: list[int]
    55→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    56→
    57→
    58→def _apply_plan_order(
    59→    items: list[dict],
    60→    plan: dict,
    61→    *,
    62→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    63→    cluster: str | None = None,
    64→) -> list[dict]:
    65→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    66→
    67→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    68→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    69→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    70→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    71→    """
    72→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    73→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    74→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    75→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    76→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    77→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup
    80→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    81→    for item in items:
    82→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    83→
    84→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    85→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    86→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    87→        if override.get("description"):
    88→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    89→        if override.get("note"):
    90→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    91→        if override.get("cluster"):
    92→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    93→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    94→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    95→                "name": cluster_name,
    96→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    97→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    98→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    99→            }
   100→
   101→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   102→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   103→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   104→    for fid in queue_order:
   105→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   106→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   107→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   108→
   109→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   110→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   111→    for item in items:
   112→        item_id = item["id"]
   113→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   114→            continue
   115→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   116→            skipped_items.append(item)
   117→        else:
   118→            remaining.append(item)
   119→
   120→    # Assign queue positions
   121→    result = ordered + remaining
   122→    if include_skipped:
   123→        result = result + skipped_items
   124→
   125→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   126→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   127→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   128→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   129→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
```
