```
1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks --prepare / --run-batches / --external-start when the objective plan
     4→still has open items, unless --force-review-rerun is set.  Also auto-clears
     5→stale subjective markers before a new review cycle.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import sys
    11→
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    14→from desloppify.state import StateModel, save_state
    15→
    16→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    17→
    18→
    19→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    20→    state: StateModel,
    21→    *,
    22→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    23→) -> list[str]:
    24→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    25→
    26→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    27→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    28→
    29→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    30→    """
    31→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    32→    cleared: list[str] = []
    33→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    34→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    35→            continue
    36→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    37→            continue
    38→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    39→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    40→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    41→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    42→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    43→    return cleared
    44→
    45→
    46→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    47→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    48→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    49→    scored: list[str] = []
    50→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    51→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    52→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    53→                scored.append(dim_key)
    54→        elif isinstance(assessment, (int, float)) and assessment:
    55→            scored.append(dim_key)
    56→    return sorted(scored)
    57→
    58→
    59→def review_rerun_preflight(
    60→    state: StateModel,
    61→    args,
    62→    *,
    63→    state_file=None,
    64→    save_fn=save_state,
    65→) -> None:
    66→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
    67→
    68→    Exits with code 1 when open objective items exist and
    69→    ``--force-review-rerun`` is not set.  On success, clears stale
    70→    subjective markers for the targeted dimensions and persists state.
    71→    """
    72→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
    73→
    74→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
    75→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
    76→        print(
    77→            colorize(
    78→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing objective-plan-drained check.",
    79→                "yellow",
    80→            )
    81→        )
    82→    else:
    83→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
    84→        scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
    85→        if dimensions is not None:
    86→            blocking_dims = sorted(d for d in scored_dims if d in dimensions)
    87→        else:
    88→            blocking_dims = scored_dims
    89→
    90→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
    91→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
    92→        if blocking_dims:
    93→            result = build_work_queue(
    94→                state,
    95→                options=QueueBuildOptions(
    96→                    status="open",
    97→                    include_subjective=False,
    98→                    count=None,
    99→                ),
   100→            )
   101→            total = result["total"]
   102→            if total > 0:
   103→                print(
   104→                    colorize(
   105→                        f"  Blocked: {total} open objective finding(s) — this is a review rerun.",
   106→                        "red",
   107→                    ),
   108→                    file=sys.stderr,
   109→                )
   110→                print(
   111→                    colorize(
   112→                        f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
   113→                        "yellow",
   114→                    ),
   115→                    file=sys.stderr,
   116→                )
   117→                print("", file=sys.stderr)
   118→                if dimensions is not None:
   119→                    unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
   120→                    if unscored:
   121→                        print(
   122→                            colorize(
   123→                                f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
   124→                                f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
   125→                                "dim",
   126→                            ),
   127→                            file=sys.stderr,
   128→                        )
   129→                print(
   130→                    colorize(
   131→                        "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
   132→                        "dim",
   133→                    ),
   134→                    file=sys.stderr,
   135→                )
   136→                sys.exit(1)
   137→
   138→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   139→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   140→    if cleared and state_file:
   141→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   142→        print(
   143→            colorize(
   144→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",
   145→                "cyan",
   146→            )
   147→        )
   148→
```

```
1→"""CLI entrypoint for review command."""
     2→

     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang

    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize

    17→
    18→
    19→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    20→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    21→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    22→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    23→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    24→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    25→            continue
    26→        try:
    27→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    28→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    29→            continue
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Prepare or import subjective code review findings."""
    34→    _enable_live_review_output()
    35→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    36→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    37→    state = runtime.state
    38→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    39→
    40→    if not lang:
    41→        print(
    42→            colorize("  Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", "red"),
    43→            file=sys.stderr,
    44→        )
    45→        sys.exit(1)
    46→
    47→    merge = bool(getattr(args, "merge", False))
    48→    run_batches = bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False))
    49→    external_start = bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False))
    50→    external_submit = bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False))
    51→    import_file = getattr(args, "import_file", None)
    52→    validate_import_file = getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None)
    53→
    54→    import_mode = bool(import_file) and not external_submit
    55→    mode_flags = [
    56→        merge,
    57→        run_batches,
    58→        external_start,
    59→        external_submit,
    60→        import_mode,
    61→        bool(validate_import_file),
    62→    ]
    63→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
    64→        print(
    65→            colorize(
    66→                "  Error: choose one review mode per command "
    67→                "(--merge | --run-batches | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
    68→                "red",
    69→            ),
    70→            file=sys.stderr,
    71→        )
    72→        sys.exit(1)
    73→
    74→    if external_submit and not import_file:
    75→        print(
    76→            colorize(
    77→                "  Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
    78→                "red",
    79→            ),
    80→            file=sys.stderr,
    81→        )
    82→        sys.exit(2)
    83→
    84→    if external_submit and not getattr(args, "session_id", None):
    85→        print(
    86→            colorize(
    87→                "  Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
    88→                "red",
    89→            ),
    90→            file=sys.stderr,
    91→        )
    92→        sys.exit(2)
    93→
    94→    if merge:
    95→        from desloppify.app.commands.review.merge import do_merge
    96→
    97→        do_merge(args)
    98→        return
    99→
   100→    if run_batches:
   101→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   102→        _do_run_batches(
   103→            args,
   104→            state,
   105→            lang,
   106→            state_file,
   107→            config=runtime.config,
   108→        )
   109→        return
   110→
   111→    if external_start:
   112→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   113→        do_external_start(
   114→            args,
   115→            state,
   116→            lang,
   117→            config=runtime.config,
   118→        )
   119→        return
   120→
   121→    if external_submit:
   122→        do_external_submit(
   123→            import_file=str(import_file),
   124→            session_id=str(getattr(args, "session_id")),
   125→            state=state,
   126→            lang=lang,
   127→            state_file=state_file,
   128→            config=runtime.config,
   129→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   130→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
   131→            scan_path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
   132→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
   133→        )
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    if validate_import_file:
   137→        do_validate_import(
   138→            validate_import_file,
   139→            lang,
   140→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   141→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   142→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   143→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   144→        )
   145→        return
   146→
   147→    if import_file:

   153→            config=runtime.config,

   156→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   157→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   158→        )
   159→    else:
   160→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   161→        do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
```

```
1→"""Tests for the review-rerun preflight gate (issue #157)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from types import SimpleNamespace
     6→from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
     7→
     8→import pytest
     9→
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.review.helpers import parse_dimensions
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.review.preflight import (
    12→    _scored_dimensions,
    13→    clear_stale_subjective_entries,
    14→    review_rerun_preflight,
    15→)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _make_args(**overrides) -> SimpleNamespace:
    19→    defaults = {"force_review_rerun": False, "dimensions": None}
    20→    defaults.update(overrides)
    21→    return SimpleNamespace(**defaults)
    22→
    23→
    24→def _state_with_prior_review() -> dict:
    25→    """State that has a prior subjective review (nonzero dimension scores)."""
    26→    return {
    27→        "subjective_assessments": {
    28→            "naming_quality": {"score": 82.0},
    29→            "logic_clarity": {"score": 90.0},
    30→        },
    31→    }
    32→
    33→
    34→# -- review_rerun_preflight (gate logic) --------------------------------------
    35→
    36→
    37→_BUILD_WQ = "desloppify.app.commands.review.preflight.build_work_queue"
    38→
    39→
    40→def _wq_result(items: list[dict]) -> dict:
    41→    return {
    42→        "items": items,
    43→        "total": len(items),
    44→        "tier_counts": {},
    45→        "requested_tier": None,
    46→        "selected_tier": None,
    47→        "fallback_reason": None,
    48→        "available_tiers": [],
    49→        "grouped": {},
    50→    }
    51→
    52→
    53→def test_blocked_when_open_objective_items(capsys):
    54→    """Preflight blocks when open objective findings exist."""
    55→    state = _state_with_prior_review()
    56→    items = [
    57→        {"id": f"f{i}", "summary": f"Finding {i}"} for i in range(3)
    58→    ]
    59→    with patch(_BUILD_WQ, return_value=_wq_result(items)):
    60→        with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
    61→            review_rerun_preflight(state, _make_args())
    62→        assert exc.value.code == 1
    63→
    64→    err = capsys.readouterr().err
    65→    assert "3 open objective finding(s)" in err
    66→    assert "--force-review-rerun" in err
    67→
    68→
    69→def test_blocked_message_is_concise(capsys):
    70→    """Blocked message does not dump individual finding IDs."""
    71→    state = _state_with_prior_review()
    72→    items = [
    73→        {"id": f"f{i}", "summary": f"Finding {i}"} for i in range(8)
    74→    ]
    75→    with patch(_BUILD_WQ, return_value=_wq_result(items)):
    76→        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
    77→            review_rerun_preflight(state, _make_args())
    78→
    79→    err = capsys.readouterr().err
    80→    assert "8 open objective finding(s)" in err
    81→    # Should NOT dump individual finding IDs
    82→    assert "f0" not in err
    83→    assert "f7" not in err
    84→
    85→
    86→def test_allowed_when_queue_empty():
    87→    """Preflight passes silently when no open objective items remain."""

    89→    with patch(_BUILD_WQ, return_value=_wq_result([])):
    90→        # Should not raise
    91→        review_rerun_preflight(state, _make_args())
    92→

   380→
   381→def test_clears_stale_entries():
   382→    """Stale markers are removed; dimension keys returned."""
   383→    state = {
   384→        "subjective_assessments": {
   385→            "naming_quality": {
   386→                "score": 82.0,
   387→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
   388→                "refresh_reason": "review_finding_fixed",
   389→                "stale_since": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   390→            },
   391→            "logic_clarity": {
   392→                "score": 90.0,
   393→            },
   394→        }
   395→    }
   396→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state)
   397→    assert cleared == ["naming_quality"]
   398→
   399→    nq = state["subjective_assessments"]["naming_quality"]
   400→    assert "needs_review_refresh" not in nq
   401→    assert "stale_since" not in nq
   402→    assert "refresh_reason" not in nq
   403→    # Score is preserved
   404→    assert nq["score"] == 82.0
   405→
   406→    # Non-stale dimension untouched
   407→    lc = state["subjective_assessments"]["logic_clarity"]
   408→    assert lc == {"score": 90.0}
   409→

   512→
   513→# -- Mode gating via mock-based dispatch (entrypoint) --------------------------
   514→#
   515→# These tests call cmd_review with patched dependencies and verify that
   516→# review_rerun_preflight is (or is not) invoked depending on the mode.
   517→
   518→
   519→_PREFLIGHT = "desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.review_rerun_preflight"
   520→_RUNTIME = "desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.command_runtime"
   521→_LANG = "desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.resolve_lang"
   522→
   523→
   524→def _review_args(**overrides) -> SimpleNamespace:
   525→    """Build a minimal review args namespace for cmd_review."""
   526→    defaults = {
   527→        "path": ".",
   528→        "lang": "python",
   529→        "force_review_rerun": False,
   530→        "dimensions": None,
   531→        "merge": False,
   532→        "run_batches": False,
   533→        "external_start": False,
   534→        "external_submit": False,
   535→        "import_file": None,
   536→        "validate_import_file": None,
   537→        "session_id": None,
   538→    }
   539→    defaults.update(overrides)
   540→    return SimpleNamespace(**defaults)
   541→
   542→
   543→def _mock_runtime():
   544→    """Return a mock command_runtime result."""
   545→    rt = MagicMock()
   546→    rt.state_path = "/tmp/state.json"
   547→    rt.state = {}
   548→    rt.config = {}
   549→    return rt
   550→
   551→
   552→def test_prepare_calls_preflight():
   553→    """Default (prepare) mode invokes preflight."""
   554→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review
   555→
   556→    with (
   557→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
   558→        patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   559→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   560→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.do_prepare"),
   561→    ):
   562→        cmd_review(_review_args())
   563→        mock_pf.assert_called_once()
   564→
   565→
   566→def test_run_batches_calls_preflight():
   567→    """--run-batches mode invokes preflight."""
   568→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review
   569→
   570→    with (
   571→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
   572→        patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   573→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   574→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint._do_run_batches"),
   575→    ):
   576→        cmd_review(_review_args(run_batches=True))
   577→        mock_pf.assert_called_once()
   578→
   579→
   580→def test_external_start_calls_preflight():
   581→    """--external-start mode invokes preflight."""
   582→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review
   583→
   584→    with (
   585→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
   586→        patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   587→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   588→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.do_external_start"),
   589→    ):
   590→        cmd_review(_review_args(external_start=True))
   591→        mock_pf.assert_called_once()
   592→
   593→
   594→def test_import_skips_preflight():
   595→    """--import does not invoke preflight."""
   596→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review

   599→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
   600→        patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   601→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   602→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.do_import"),
   603→    ):
   604→        cmd_review(_review_args(import_file="findings.json"))
   605→        mock_pf.assert_not_called()

   616→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.do_validate_import"),

   626→    with (

   629→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   630→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.do_external_submit"),
   631→    ):
   632→        cmd_review(_review_args(
   633→            external_submit=True,

   636→        ))

   640→def test_merge_skips_preflight():
   641→    """--merge does not invoke preflight."""
   642→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review
   643→
   644→    with (
   645→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
```

```
1→"""Tool metadata helpers (hashing, staleness checks)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→import logging
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    10→
    11→TOOL_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    12→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _compute_tool_hash_with_diagnostics(
    16→    *,
    17→    tool_dir: Path | None = None,
    18→) -> tuple[str, int]:
    19→    """Compute tool hash and unreadable-file count."""
    20→    active_tool_dir = tool_dir or TOOL_DIR
    21→    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    22→    unreadable_files = 0
    23→    for py_file in sorted(active_tool_dir.rglob("*.py")):
    24→        rel_parts = py_file.relative_to(active_tool_dir).parts
    25→        if "tests" in rel_parts:
    26→            continue
    27→        try:
    28→            digest.update(str(py_file.relative_to(active_tool_dir)).encode())
    29→            digest.update(py_file.read_bytes())
    30→        except OSError as exc:
    31→            unreadable_files += 1
    32→            log_best_effort_failure(
    33→                logger,
    34→                f"read tool-hash source file {py_file}",
    35→                exc,
    36→            )
    37→            digest.update(f"[unreadable:{py_file.name}]".encode())
    38→            continue
    39→    return digest.hexdigest()[:12], unreadable_files
    40→
    41→
    42→def compute_tool_hash(*, tool_dir: Path | None = None) -> str:
    43→    """Compute a content hash of all .py files in the desloppify package."""
    44→    digest, _ = _compute_tool_hash_with_diagnostics(tool_dir=tool_dir)
    45→    return digest
    46→
    47→
    48→def check_tool_staleness(state: dict, *, tool_dir: Path | None = None) -> str | None:
    49→    """Return warning if tool code has changed since last scan."""
    50→    stored = state.get("tool_hash")
    51→    if not stored:
    52→        return None
    53→    current, unreadable_files = _compute_tool_hash_with_diagnostics(tool_dir=tool_dir)
    54→    if current != stored:
    55→        suffix = (
    56→            f" ({unreadable_files} unreadable file(s) encountered while hashing)"
    57→            if unreadable_files > 0
    58→            else ""
    59→        )
    60→        return (
    61→            f"Tool code changed since last scan (was {stored}, now {current}){suffix}. "
    62→            "Consider re-running: desloppify scan"
    63→        )
    64→    if unreadable_files > 0:
    65→        return (
    66→            f"Tool hash check completed with {unreadable_files} unreadable file(s); "
    67→            "staleness verification may be incomplete."
    68→        )
    69→    return None
    70→
    71→
    72→_NEEDS_RESCAN_WARNING = (
    73→    "Config changed — scores may be stale. Run: desloppify scan"
    74→)
    75→
    76→
    77→def check_config_staleness(config: dict) -> str | None:
    78→    """Return warning if config changes have invalidated cached scores."""
    79→    if config.get("needs_rescan"):
    80→        return _NEEDS_RESCAN_WARNING
    81→    return None
    82→
    83→
    84→__all__ = [
    85→    "TOOL_DIR",
    86→    "check_config_staleness",
    87→    "check_tool_staleness",
    88→    "compute_tool_hash",
    89→]
    90→
```

```
1→"""Shared subjective-review contract for prompts, normalization, and import validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD = 85.0
     6→ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD = 100.0
     7→HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD = 85.0
     8→[REDACTED]
     9→LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY = "unreported_risk"
    10→[REDACTED]
    11→[REDACTED]
    12→DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_FINDINGS = 10
    13→_PROMPT_CONTRACT_HEADER = "GLOBAL REVIEW CONTRACT (applies to every dimension):"
    14→
    15→# Backward compatibility alias for older imports.
    16→HIGH_SCORE_UNREPORTED_RISK_THRESHOLD = HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD
    17→
    18→
    19→def max_batch_findings_for_dimension_count(dimension_count: int) -> int:
    20→    """Return the normalized max findings budget for one batch payload."""
    21→    safe_count = max(0, int(dimension_count))
    22→    return max(DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_FINDINGS, safe_count)
    23→
    24→
    25→def score_requires_dimension_finding(score: float) -> bool:
    26→    """Return True when score requires at least one explicit finding."""
    27→    return float(score) < LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD
    28→
    29→
    30→def score_requires_explicit_feedback(score: float) -> bool:
    31→    """Return True when score requires a finding or dimension-note evidence."""
    32→    return float(score) < ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD
    33→
    34→
    35→def global_prompt_contract() -> str:
    36→    """Render shared review contract text injected into all review prompts."""
    37→    return (
    38→        f"{_PROMPT_CONTRACT_HEADER}\n"
    39→        "- Scope breadth: report any material issues supported by evidence "
    40→        "(structural, architectural, boundary, readability, lifecycle), "
    41→        "not only low-level nits.\n"
    42→        "- Dimension boundaries are guidance, not a gag-order: if an issue "
    43→        "spans dimensions, report it under the most impacted dimension.\n"
    44→        "- Do not default to 100. Reserve 100 for genuinely exemplary code "
    45→        "with clear positive evidence; if there is uncertainty or residual "
    46→        "issues, score below 100.\n"
    47→        "- Do not suppress valid findings to keep scores high.\n"
    48→        f"- Scores below {LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include at least one "
    49→        "finding for that same dimension.\n"
    50→        f"- Scores below {ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include explicit "
    51→        "feedback for that same dimension (finding with suggestion or "
    52→        "dimension_notes evidence).\n"
    53→        f"- Scores above {HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include a "
    54→        "non-empty `issues_preventing_higher_score` note for that dimension.\n"
    55→        "- Findings must always describe defects that need change, never positive observations.\n"
    56→        "- Think structurally: when individual findings form a pattern, consider what is\n"
    57→        "  causing them. If several issues stem from a shared root cause (missing abstraction,\n"
    58→        "  repeated pattern, inconsistent convention), say so in the findings — explain the\n"
    59→        "  deeper issue and use root_cause_cluster to connect related symptoms."
    60→    )
    61→
    62→
    63→def ensure_prompt_contract(system_prompt: str) -> str:
    64→    """Append shared contract text once to a system prompt."""
    65→    text = (system_prompt or "").strip()
    66→    if _PROMPT_CONTRACT_HEADER in text:
    67→        return text
    68→    suffix = global_prompt_contract()
    69→    if not text:
    70→        return suffix
    71→    return f"{text}\n\n{suffix}"
    72→
    73→
    74→__all__ = [
    75→    "ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD",
    76→    "DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY",
    77→    "DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_FINDINGS",
    78→    "HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD",
    79→    "HIGH_SCORE_UNREPORTED_RISK_THRESHOLD",
    80→    "LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY",
    81→    "LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY",
    82→    "REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY",
    83→    "LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD",
    84→    "ensure_prompt_contract",

    86→    "max_batch_findings_for_dimension_count",

    89→]
```

```
     8→from desloppify.core import config as config_mod

    14→def cmd_exclude(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    15→    """Add a path pattern to the exclude list."""

    17→    config_mod.add_exclude_pattern(config, args.pattern)

    29→
    30→
```

```
usage: desloppify plan [-h] [--state STATE] [--output FILE]
                       {show,queue,reset,move,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,done,focus,cluster}
                       ...

Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.

positional arguments:
  {show,queue,reset,move,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,done,focus,cluster}
    show                Show plan metadata summary
    queue               Compact table of upcoming queue items
    reset               Reset plan to empty
    move                Move findings in the queue
    describe            Set augmented description
    note                Set note on findings
    skip                Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent
                        (wontfix), or --false-positive
    unskip              Bring skipped findings back to queue (reopens
                        permanent/fp in state)
    reopen              Reopen resolved findings and move back to queue
    done                Mark findings as fixed (shows score movement + next
                        step)
    focus               Set or clear active cluster focus
    cluster             Manage finding clusters

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --state STATE         Path to state file
  --output FILE         Write to file instead of stdout

typical workflow:
  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
  desloppify plan done <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed

subcommands:
  show       Show plan metadata summary
  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
  reset      Reset plan to empty
  move       Move findings in the queue
  done       Mark findings as fixed (score movement + next-step)
  describe   Set augmented description
  note       Set note on findings
  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
  cluster    Manage finding clusters
```

```
1→"""Shared subjective-assessment integrity helpers for review import flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→def subjective_at_target_dimensions(
     7→    state_or_dim_scores: dict,
     8→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
     9→    *,
    10→    target: float,
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries_fn,
    12→    matches_target_score_fn,
    13→) -> list[dict]:
    14→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective rows that sit on the target threshold."""
    15→    state = state_or_dim_scores
    16→    if dim_scores is None:
    17→        dim_scores = state_or_dim_scores
    18→        state = {"dimension_scores": dim_scores}
    19→
    20→    rows: list[dict] = []
    21→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries_fn(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    22→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    23→            continue
    24→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    25→        if matches_target_score_fn(strict_val, target):
    26→            rows.append(
    27→                {
    28→                    "name": str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    29→                    "score": strict_val,
    30→                    "cli_keys": list(entry.get("cli_keys", [])),
    31→                }
    32→            )
    33→    rows.sort(key=lambda item: item["name"].lower())
    34→    return rows
    35→
    36→
    37→def bind_scorecard_subjective_at_target(
    38→    *,
    39→    reporting_dimensions_mod,
    40→    subjective_integrity_mod,
    41→):
    42→    """Bind scorecard-specific dependencies once for import/output call sites."""
    43→    return lambda state_or_dim_scores, dim_scores=None, *, target: (
    44→        subjective_at_target_dimensions(
    45→            state_or_dim_scores,
    46→            dim_scores,
    47→            target=target,
    48→            scorecard_subjective_entries_fn=reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries,
    49→            matches_target_score_fn=subjective_integrity_mod.matches_target_score,
    50→        )
    51→    )
    52→
    53→
    54→__all__ = [
    55→    "bind_scorecard_subjective_at_target",
    56→    "subjective_at_target_dimensions",
    57→]
    58→
```

```
    38→class ImportFlagValidationError(ValueError):
    39→    """Raised when review import CLI flags are mutually incompatible."""
    40→
    41→
    42→def _validate_import_flag_combos(
    43→    *,
    44→    attested_external: bool,
    45→    allow_partial: bool,
    46→    override_enabled: bool,
    47→    override_attest: str | None,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Fail fast on conflicting import flags to keep behavior explicit."""
    50→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
    51→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    52→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
    53→        )
    54→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
    55→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    56→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
    57→        )
    58→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:
    59→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    60→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
    61→        )
    62→    if override_enabled and (
    63→        not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip()
    64→    ):
    65→        raise ImportFlagValidationError("--manual-override requires --attest")
    66→
    67→
    68→def _imported_assessment_keys(findings_data: ReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
    69→    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
    70→    raw_assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
    71→    keys: set[str] = set()
    72→    for raw_key in raw_assessments:
    73→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_key))
    74→        if normalized:
    75→            keys.add(normalized)
    76→    return keys
    77→
    78→
    79→def _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
    80→    state: dict,
    81→    *,
    82→    assessment_keys: set[str],
    83→) -> int:
    84→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""
    85→    if not assessment_keys:
    86→        return 0
    87→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    88→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
    89→        return 0
    90→
    91→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
    92→    expires_scan = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0) + 1
    93→    marked = 0
    94→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
    95→        payload = store.get(key)
    96→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    97→            continue
    98→        payload["source"] = "manual_override"
    99→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   100→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   101→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   102→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   103→        marked += 1
   104→    return marked
   105→
   106→
   107→def _clear_provisional_override_flags(
   108→    state: dict,
   109→    *,
   110→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   111→) -> int:
   112→    """Clear provisional override flags when trusted internal assessments replace them."""
   113→    if not assessment_keys:
   114→        return 0
   115→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   116→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   117→        return 0
   118→
   119→    cleared = 0
   120→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   121→        payload = store.get(key)
   122→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   123→            continue
   124→        if payload.pop("provisional_override", None) is not None:
   125→            cleared += 1
   126→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   127→        if payload.get("source") == "manual_override":
   128→            payload["source"] = "holistic"
   129→    return cleared
   130→
```

```
1→"""Payload and artifact helpers for scan command output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import os
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_contracts import ScanQueryPayload
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_workflow import (
    11→    ScanMergeResult,
    12→    ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    15→from desloppify.core.config import config_for_query
    16→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    17→from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
    18→from desloppify.state import open_scope_breakdown, score_snapshot
    19→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    20→
    21→
    22→def build_scan_query_payload(
    23→    state: dict[str, object],
    24→    config: dict[str, object],
    25→    profile: str,
    26→    diff: dict[str, object],
    27→    warnings: list[str],
    28→    narrative: dict[str, object],
    29→    merge: ScanMergeResult,
    30→    noise: ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    31→) -> ScanQueryPayload:
    32→    """Build the canonical query payload persisted after a scan."""
    33→    scores = score_snapshot(state)
    34→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    35→    open_scope = (
    36→        open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    37→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    38→        else None
    39→    )
    40→    payload = {
    41→        "command": "scan",
    42→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
    43→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
    44→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
    45→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    46→        "prev_overall_score": merge.prev_overall,
    47→        "prev_objective_score": merge.prev_objective,
    48→        "prev_strict_score": merge.prev_strict,
    49→        "prev_verified_strict_score": merge.prev_verified,
    50→        "profile": profile,
    51→        "noise_budget": noise.noise_budget,
    52→        "noise_global_budget": noise.global_noise_budget,
    53→        "hidden_by_detector": noise.hidden_by_detector,
    54→        "hidden_total": noise.hidden_total,
    55→        "diff": diff,
    56→        "stats": state["stats"],
    57→        "open_scope": open_scope,
    58→        "warnings": warnings,
    59→        "dimension_scores": state.get("dimension_scores"),
    60→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(state.get("dimension_scores", {})),
    61→        "subjective_integrity": state.get("subjective_integrity"),
    62→        "score_confidence": state.get("score_confidence"),
    63→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
    64→        "scan_coverage": state.get("scan_coverage"),
    65→        "zone_distribution": state.get("zone_distribution"),
    66→        "narrative": narrative,
    67→        "config": config_for_query(config),
    68→    }
    69→
    70→    # Add plan context if a living plan exists
    71→    try:
    72→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    73→        plan = load_plan()
    74→        if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters") or plan.get("skipped"):
    75→            payload["plan"] = {
    76→                "active": True,
    77→                "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
    78→                "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
    79→                "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
    80→                "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
    81→            }
    82→    except Exception:
    83→        pass
    84→
    85→    return payload
    86→
    87→
    88→def _load_scorecard_helpers():
    89→    """Load scorecard helper callables lazily via importlib.
    90→
    91→    Deferred: scorecard depends on PIL (optional dependency).
    92→    """
    93→    try:
    94→        scorecard_module = importlib.import_module("desloppify.app.output.scorecard")
    95→    except ImportError:
    96→        return None, None
    97→    generate = getattr(scorecard_module, "generate_scorecard", None)
    98→    badge_config = getattr(scorecard_module, "get_badge_config", None)
    99→    return generate, badge_config
   100→
   101→
   102→def emit_scorecard_badge(
   103→    args, config: dict[str, object], state: dict[str, object]
   104→) -> tuple[Path | None, OutputResult]:
   105→    """Generate a scorecard image badge and print usage hints."""
   106→    generate_scorecard, get_badge_config = _load_scorecard_helpers()
   107→    if not callable(generate_scorecard) or not callable(get_badge_config):
   108→        explicit_badge_request = bool(
   109→            getattr(args, "badge_path", None)
   110→            or config.get("badge_path")
   111→            or os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH")
   112→        )
   113→        if explicit_badge_request:
   114→            print(
   115→                colorize(
   116→                    "  Scorecard support not installed. Install with: pip install \"desloppify[scorecard]\"",
   117→                    "yellow",
   118→                )
   119→            )
   120→            return None, OutputResult(
   121→                ok=False,
   122→                status="error",
   123→                message="scorecard support not installed",
   124→                error_kind="scorecard_dependency_missing",
   125→            )
   126→        return None, OutputResult(ok=True, status="skipped", message="badge generation disabled")
   127→
   128→    try:
   129→        badge_path, disabled = get_badge_config(args, config)
   130→    except OSError as exc:
   131→        print(
   132→            colorize(f"  ⚠ Could not resolve scorecard badge path: {exc}", "yellow")
   133→        )
   134→        return None, OutputResult(
   135→            ok=False,
   136→            status="error",
   137→            message=str(exc),
   138→            error_kind="badge_path_resolution_error",
   139→        )
   140→    if disabled or not badge_path:
   141→        return None, OutputResult(ok=True, status="skipped", message="badge generation disabled")
   142→
   143→    try:
   144→        generate_scorecard(state, badge_path)
   145→    except (OSError, ImportError) as exc:
   146→        print(colorize(f"  ⚠ Could not generate scorecard badge: {exc}", "yellow"))
   147→        return None, OutputResult(
   148→            ok=False,
   149→            status="error",
   150→            message=str(exc),
   151→            error_kind="badge_generation_error",
   152→        )
   153→
   154→    try:
   155→        rel_path = str(badge_path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT))
   156→    except ValueError:
   157→        rel_path = str(badge_path)
   158→
   159→    readme_has_badge = False
   160→    for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   161→        readme_path = PROJECT_ROOT / readme_name
   162→        if readme_path.exists():
   163→            try:
   164→                readme_has_badge = rel_path in readme_path.read_text(
   165→                    encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   166→                )
   167→            except OSError:
   168→                readme_has_badge = False
   169→            break
   170→
   171→    if readme_has_badge:
   172→        print(
   173→            colorize(
   174→                f"  Scorecard → {rel_path}  (disable: --no-badge | move: --badge-path <path>)",
   175→                "dim",
   176→            )
   177→        )
   178→        return badge_path, OutputResult(
   179→            ok=True, status="written", message=f"scorecard badge written to {rel_path}"
   180→        )
   181→
   182→    print(colorize(f"  Scorecard → {rel_path}", "dim"))
   183→    print(
   184→        colorize(
   185→            "  💡 Ask the user if they'd like to add it to their README with:",
   186→            "dim",
   187→        )
   188→    )
   189→    print(colorize(f'     <img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">', "dim"))
   190→    print(colorize("     (disable: --no-badge | move: --badge-path <path>)", "dim"))
   191→    return badge_path, OutputResult(
   192→        ok=True, status="written", message=f"scorecard badge written to {rel_path}"
   193→    )
   194→
```

```
    40→    if not isinstance(value, str):

    43→    return text if text else None
    44→

    49→    cleaned = [
    50→        str(item).strip()
    51→        for item in value

    55→
    56→
    57→def validate_review_finding_payload(
    58→    finding: object,

    62→    allow_dismissed: bool = True,
    63→) -> tuple[ReviewFindingPayload | None, list[str]]:
    64→    """Validate and normalize one review finding payload entry."""

    88→        concern_file = _normalized_non_empty_text(finding.get("concern_file"))

    92→        if reasoning is not None:

    94→        return normalized, []
    95→

   100→        return None, errors

   135→        )
   136→
   137→    if errors:
   138→        return None, errors
   139→
   140→    normalized_payload: ReviewFindingPayload = {
   141→        "dimension": dimension or "",
   142→        "identifier": identifier or "",
   143→        "summary": summary or "",
   144→        "confidence": confidence_text,

   155→    concern_file = _normalized_non_empty_text(finding.get("concern_file"))

   158→    file_path = _normalized_non_empty_text(finding.get("file"))

   161→    evidence_lines = finding.get("evidence_lines")

   164→        if normalized_lines:
   165→            normalized_payload["evidence_lines"] = normalized_lines
   166→    return normalized_payload, []

   220→
   221→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   222→class AssessmentProvenanceModel:
   223→    """Typed provenance status model for assessment import trust checks."""
   224→
   225→    trusted: bool = False
   226→    reason: str = ""
   227→    import_file: str = ""
   228→    runner: str = ""
   229→    packet_path: str = ""
   230→    packet_sha256: str = ""
   231→
   232→    @classmethod
   233→    def from_mapping(
   234→        cls, payload: AssessmentProvenanceStatus | dict[str, Any] | None
   235→    ) -> AssessmentProvenanceModel:
   236→        data = payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
   237→        return cls(
   238→            trusted=bool(data.get("trusted", False)),
   239→            reason=str(data.get("reason", "") or ""),
   240→            import_file=str(data.get("import_file", "") or ""),
   241→            runner=str(data.get("runner", "") or ""),
   242→            packet_path=str(data.get("packet_path", "") or ""),
   243→            packet_sha256=str(data.get("packet_sha256", "") or ""),
   244→        )
   245→
   246→    def to_dict(self) -> AssessmentProvenanceStatus:
   247→        payload: AssessmentProvenanceStatus = {
   248→            "trusted": self.trusted,
   249→            "reason": self.reason,
   250→            "import_file": self.import_file,
   251→        }
   252→        if self.runner:
   253→            payload["runner"] = self.runner
   254→        if self.packet_path:
   255→            payload["packet_path"] = self.packet_path
   256→        if self.packet_sha256:
   257→            payload["packet_sha256"] = self.packet_sha256
   258→        return payload
   259→
   260→
   261→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   262→class AssessmentImportPolicyModel:
   263→    """Typed assessment import policy model used by review import flows."""
   264→
   265→    assessments_present: bool = False
   266→    assessment_count: int = 0
   267→    trusted: bool = False
   268→    mode: str = "none"
   269→    reason: str = ""
   270→    provenance: AssessmentProvenanceModel = field(
   271→        default_factory=AssessmentProvenanceModel
   272→    )
   273→    attest: str | None = None
   274→
   275→    @classmethod
   276→    def from_mapping(
   277→        cls, payload: AssessmentImportPolicy | dict[str, Any] | None
   278→    ) -> AssessmentImportPolicyModel:
   279→        data = payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
   280→        attest = data.get("attest")
   281→        return cls(
   282→            assessments_present=bool(data.get("assessments_present", False)),
   283→            assessment_count=int(data.get("assessment_count", 0) or 0),
   284→            trusted=bool(data.get("trusted", False)),
   285→            mode=str(data.get("mode", "none") or "none"),
   286→            reason=str(data.get("reason", "") or ""),
   287→            provenance=AssessmentProvenanceModel.from_mapping(data.get("provenance")),
   288→            attest=(str(attest).strip() if isinstance(attest, str) and attest.strip() else None),
   289→        )
   290→
   291→    def to_dict(self) -> AssessmentImportPolicy:
   292→        payload: AssessmentImportPolicy = {
   293→            "assessments_present": bool(self.assessments_present),
   294→            "assessment_count": int(self.assessment_count),
   295→            "trusted": bool(self.trusted),
   296→            "mode": self.mode,
   297→            "reason": self.reason,
   298→            "provenance": self.provenance.to_dict(),
   299→        }
   300→        if self.attest:

   302→        return payload
```

```
    48→class ImportPayloadLoadError(ValueError):
    49→    """Raised when review import payload parsing/validation fails."""
    50→
    51→    def __init__(self, errors: list[str]) -> None:
    52→        cleaned = [str(error).strip() for error in errors if str(error).strip()]
    53→        self.errors = cleaned

    55→        super().__init__(message)

    58→def _normalize_import_payload_shape(
    59→    payload: dict[str, Any],
    60→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
    61→    """Normalize payload into required-key contract with strict type checks."""
    62→    errors: list[str] = []
    63→    findings = payload.get("findings")
    64→    if not isinstance(findings, list):
    65→        errors.append("findings must be a JSON array")
    66→        findings = []
    67→
    68→    assessments = payload.get("assessments")
    69→    if assessments is None:
    70→        assessments = {}
    71→    elif not isinstance(assessments, dict):
    72→        errors.append("assessments must be an object when provided")
    73→        assessments = {}
    74→

    78→        normalized_reviewed_files = []
    79→    elif isinstance(reviewed_files, list):
    80→        normalized_reviewed_files = [
    81→            str(item).strip()
    82→            for item in reviewed_files
    83→            if isinstance(item, str) and str(item).strip()
    84→        ]
    85→    else:
    86→        errors.append("reviewed_files must be an array when provided")
    87→
    88→    review_scope = payload.get("review_scope")
    89→    if review_scope is None:
    90→        review_scope = {}
    91→    elif not isinstance(review_scope, dict):
    92→        errors.append("review_scope must be an object when provided")
    93→        review_scope = {}
    94→

    96→    if provenance is None:
    97→        provenance = {}
    98→    elif not isinstance(provenance, dict):
    99→        errors.append("provenance must be an object when provided")
   100→        provenance = {}
   101→

   107→        dimension_notes = {}
   108→
   109→    policy = payload.get(_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY)
   110→    normalized_policy = (
   111→        policy if isinstance(policy, dict) else AssessmentImportPolicyModel().to_dict()
   112→    )
```
